# Product lines, sub-brands, and bases

Posters often type the LINE name alone. Lines and soap bases behave
like knot generations: identity, not decoration.

- **[catalog] Mitchell's Wool Fat — formula eras (established
  2026-10-22).** One product (Shaving Soap, refill/ceramic/metal/wooden
  bowls), TWO formulas = era attribute, never distinct entries:
  **tallow era** (recipe dating to 1893; beef tallow + lanolin; through
  ~fall 2022) → **tallow-free era** (2023 version, then improved 2024
  version; palmate-based; lanolin RETAINED). Validators: dataset strings
  that say "old formula"/"old puck"/"pre-reformulation" = tallow era;
  2023+ purchases without qualification = tallow-free. Kent shaving
  soap = the same puck in Kent wrapping (community consensus; see
  lineages.md) — BUT era-qualified per the 2026-10-27 addendum:
  pre-2023 Kent = MWF; 2023–2024 Kent = alternative-supplier
  sorbitol formulation (Kent website ~Apr 2024); end-2024 Amazon-US
  Kent cakes = MWF second-palmate list again (channel split
  unresolved). Validators: never fold a 2023+ Kent string into MWF
  without ingredient/batch evidence.
- **[catalog] Wholly Kaw — base progression (established 2026-10-21/22).**
  Posters treat the base as an identity tag, so base-era is an attribute
  on the product entry, not a separate product. Sequence:
  **Vegan** (launch ~2014–15 — Sharpologist ~Oct 2015: "a vegan shaving
  products maker, that released its first soaps in roughly the last
  year"; formula "fairly similar to" LASSCO/Catie's Bubbles) →
  **Donkey Milk** ("WKDM" shorthand, ~2018; tallow + donkey milk) →
  **Bufala** (~2019; adds water buffalo milk — B&B "Wholly Kaw fans" p.2:
  "new Bufala soaps") → **Siero** (~2021; "siero" = whey in Italian —
  donkey-milk whey + water buffalo-milk whey proteins; Maggard copy
  spells out the full composition; Sharpologist Bare Siero matchup ~Nov
  2021) → **Crème Fraîche** (newest; in the founder's 2026 lineup
  statement alongside Tallow, Bufala, Siero). KEY BASE FACT: all four
  dairy bases are beef-tallow + WHOLE donkey milk per the founder's
  on-record statement (~Aug 2026) — "Tallow", "Bufala", "Siero",
  "Crème Fraîche" differ by dairy additions, not by tallow-vs-dairy.
  The vegan line is formulated separately (no beef tallow, no animal
  milk or whey). Validators: a bare "Wholly Kaw - <scent>" string without
  a base tag usually means whatever the current base was when posted —
  do NOT default it to Siero or Tallow (cf. P-11 no-false-precision);
  "Bufala"/"Siero"/"WKDM" strings carry the era. Naming convention:
  the fougère-adjacent **\*Concerto** series (Fern Concerto, La Fougère
  Parfaite, Chypre Rose Concerto — Daily Lather Dec 2016; B&B 2018).
  Sources: Sharpologist ~Oct 2015 + ~Nov 2021 reviews; B&B 539727,
  560031 p.2, 547986; Maggard product copy (Siero composition);
  Prism News founder statement (~Aug 2026); whollykaw.com (live).

- **Cold River Soap Works — lines & bases** [catalog]. Four named
  bases/lines over its life (closed ~2021–2023): **Select** (original
  tallow line; **Select V2** = revised tallow formula, early 2016 —
  posters specify "Select" vs "Select V2"), **Premium** (a 2017 line
  named in dataset notes: "new CRSW Premium formula"), **Oliva**
  (olive-oil vegan line launched early 2016; scents Colonia
  Mediterrania, Honeywood, Unscented), and **Glide** (later tallow
  base, the one posters praise as "stringy proto lather" — used for
  Morning Ghost, *Brazen Moment*, and the BullGoose "Cold River X"
  exclusive). Posters also cite seasonal LE runs (Spring/Summer/Fall
  Limited Edition). Sources: Sharpologist Oliva review (2016, gives
  the Select V2 vs Oliva formula comparison); dataset notes 2017
  (Premium), 2019, 2024 (Glide). Confidence: established.

- **Seaforth!** is the revived vintage brand line of **Spearhead Shaving
  Co.** (Sea Spice Lime etc. are Spearhead products). Source: catalog
  soap research 2026-10-20. Established. [catalog]
- **[catalog] Black Ship Grooming — "Kaito" is a BASE, not a scent
  (established 2026-10-24; corrects the 2026-10-21 lineages entry).**
  Posters write it base-first: "Black Ship Grooming Kaito -
  Fitzgerald" / "Kaito - Whydah" (sgrdddy, July 2026). Earlier
  evidence: 2021-06-15 (namziah) "the vegan base (Kaito, I think it's
  called) of Captain Scurvy"; 2022-01-23 (wyze0ne) "this 'Kaito' base
  is legit af" (Hoptoberfest); the maker's Scrooge description calls
  it the "New Kaito shaving cream base." Validators: NEVER create a
  "Kaito" scent entry, and never parse "Kaito - <scent>" as a scent
  name — Kaito is the base attribute on the scent's entry, exactly
  like the GD scent+base key. The Final Voyage pair in Kaito base =
  Fitzgerald + Whydah (see lineages.md correction).

  **ADDENDUM 2026-10-26 — the full base lineup, answering the
  backlog's open questions (dataset mining, sotd_2016–2026_full.json
  + maker blog + Bladetester).** Kaito's launch bracket is now
  **by February 2021**: the earliest dataset Kaito tag is
  2021-02-22 (WolfSK-88, soap field "Black Ship Grooming - Stolen
  Heart kaito base", bought "as soon as I could" = new release),
  and the name was still settling in mid-2021 (namziah 2021-06-15:
  "the vegan base (Kaito, I think it's called)"). Kaito-base
  scents attested in the dataset: **Stolen Heart (2021), Captain
  Scurvy vegan version (2021), Hoptoberfest (2022), Scrooge
  (2022, maker description), Fitzgerald + Whydah (2026, Final
  Voyage)** — plus Crane titled "Shaving Cream" (fall 2025).
  **Kaito is NOT Black Ship's first vegan base.** The ORIGINAL
  house base was already vegan: 2018-03-15 (reguyw_nothingtolose)
  "Two Bits is vegan"; 2019-04-04 / 2019-05-04 (Not_a_robot_101)
  Philip's Fougere "vegan, built around a coconut and shea butter
  base"; 2019-10-06 (Aresmsu) White Whale "as far as vegan soaps
  go"; 2023-07-12 (AdWorried2804, Davy Jones) "BSG's original soap
  base ... consists of Coconut oil, Shea Butter, Stearic Acid,
  Water, Potassium Hydroxide, Glycerin"; Bladetester corroborates
  the coconut/shea/glycerin profile for Siren's Song. So Kaito is
  the maker's NEW/SECOND vegan base (a reformulation or renamed
  line, ~2020–2021), and some scents exist in both eras
  (Captain Scurvy, Captain's Reserve, Cap'n Darkside all carry
  trythatsoap soap_vegan SKUs). **"Triple butter" is maker
  marketing, NOT a separate base or line**: the maker's blog
  ("The Art of Smooth Shaves") calls the soap collection "Triple
  butter shaving soaps" and says "the triple butter blend and
  tallow provide hydration" — but NO poster-quoted ingredient
  list for any Black Ship soap contains tallow, and the
  "original base" list above is tallow-free. Treat the blog's
  tallow phrase as uncorroborated marketing copy: do NOT create
  a Black Ship tallow-base entry on it. **"Shaving cream base"
  is texture language, not a format split**: the maker calls
  Kaito a "shaving cream base" and titles Crane "Shaving Cream",
  yet Fitzgerald (titled "Shaving Soap") is Kaito-base per
  posters — validators must not split soap vs cream entries on
  the title word. **Outlier (single-reviewer lead, not a house
  base):** Bladetester lists Christmas Cannons at "argan oil,
  lanolin, sunflower oil" — lanolin would make it non-vegan, so
  if true it is a seasonal-variant formula, not the house base;
  needs a label photo before any catalog use. Confidence:
  established (Kaito = new vegan base, launch by Feb 2021;
  original base = vegan coconut/shea; triple-butter = marketing;
  no evidenced tallow base); the exact Kaito launch announcement
  and the Christmas Cannons lanolin note remain leads.

  **ADDENDUM 2026-10-26 (Wayback Scrooge read landed):** the
  captured Scrooge page (nearest capture to the requested
  2026-10-22 snapshot is 2024-04-22; only 3 captures exist for
  that URL, Mar 2023–Apr 2024; the listing itself dates to late
  2022 per image timestamps) confirms the maker copy calls it
  the "New Kaito shaving cream base" — so "new" was still the
  maker's wording in the 2024 capture, consistent with Kaito
  being the new base in 2021–22 rather than a later rename. The
  capture's "Ingredients:" section is EMPTY (no ingredient text
  rendered), so it adds no ingredient evidence either way. The
  page titles the product a shaving soap while calling its base
  a shaving cream base — confirming the soap/cream wording split
  is maker copy, not a format distinction. Scent notes for
  Scrooge: black pepper, balsams, bergamot, lavender; the
  Christmas Carol collection siblings named on the page: Three
  Ghosts of Christmas, The Golden Carol, Wassail, Silent Knight,
  Fezziwig, Scrooge.
- **Barrister and Mann**: the **Reserve** line is distinct from the
  core line; **Hallows** (Halloween) and **Dickens** (Christmas) are
  recurring seasonal releases. Source: catalog work 2026-10-20.
  Established. [catalog]
- **Declaration Grooming** soaps are known by their **Milksteak** base;
  posters cite the base when comparing eras (L&L-era vs Milksteak).
  Source: catalog work 2026-10-20. Confidence: lead → verify against
  maker sources. [catalog]
- **Barrister and Mann — Barrister's Reserve®: line AND base, resolved
  2026-10-21** [catalog]. "Reserve" is both a product LINE name and
  the name of the lanolin-free soap BASE exclusive to that line.
  Maker FAQ (quoted on Badger & Blade): Reserve soaps "are offered
  only in the Barrister's Reserve® scents (Classic, Cool, Spice,
  Lavender, Fern, and Waves) and are restricted specifically to that
  line." Line launched **June 9, 2017** (soap $17.99; matching
  splashes); beta formula debuted in the one-off **Soapmakers of
  Awesometown** (Apr 1, 2017). Launch scents (Jun 2017): **Classic,
  Cool, Spice**; **Fern** and **Lavender** added Sep/Dec 2017;
  **Waves** added Nov 2018. All six are recreations of vintage
  fragrances (Spice = 1957 Shulton Old Spice; Classic = Gillette
  Sun Up; Cool = Flöid Blue; Lavender = Caldey Island Lavender;
  Fern = Houbigant Fougère Royale). Base exceptions: Fougère
  Gothique (Oct 2017) and a one-off Reddit-exclusive Seville used
  the Reserve base without joining the line. Sources: Daily Lather
  "History of Barrister and Mann" (updated 2025-05-05) Reserve
  table; Daily Lather review 2017-06-09; B&B threads 527966,
  587797, 621964. Confidence: established.
- **Barrister and Mann — Omnibus transition (fall 2021)** [catalog].
  Will Carius discontinued the Reserve, Excelsior, and Soft Heart
  bases and consolidated on the new **Omnibus** base (debut: the
  fall 2021 Leviathan drop). The Reserve SCENTS survived: they were
  re-released in Omnibus (Cool first, late 2021; Spice followed),
  and the maker DROPPED "Reserve" from the product names — e.g. the
  soap is now sold simply as **"Spice Shaving Soap"** at the SAME
  product URL whose slug still reads
  `barristers-reserve-spice-shaving-soap` (live page fetched
  2026-10-21: title "Spice Shaving Soap", $24.99, Omnibus
  ingredient list, scent copy verbatim from 2017: carnation,
  nutmeg, vanilla, tonka). Retailer listings render it "Barrister
  and Mann Spice Shaving Soap (Omnibus Base)" (Naked Armor).
  Sources: B&B thread 613527 (Omnibus replacement announcement
  discussion, 2021); B&B thread 551830 ("Reserve Cool is already
  in Omnibus", late 2021); barristerandmann.com product page;
  dataset strings 2022+ ("B&M Reserve Spice (omnibus)", "Spice
  (Omnibus)"). Confidence: established.

- **Dr. Jon's Handcrafted Soap Co. — formula eras & lines (closed
  2022)** [catalog]. ALL-vegan formulas. **Volume 1** (2015–2016 era) =
  extremely soft "croap" — so soft posters treated it like a cream
  (scoop-and-lather; thread debates that the softness was intended for
  dense protective lather). **Volume 2** reformulation (~2017) =
  firmer, less water-hungry (tailorandbarber Vol 2 review). Posters
  don't consistently mark "Vol. 1/2," so catalog entries should treat
  volume as an era attribute, not a distinct product. Scents known to
  span both eras. Named lines/products (all soaps + matching aftershave
  "tonics"; later EdT for some): Classic, Hydra, Arctic Hydra, Black
  Label, Gold Label, Big Green Monster, Anne Bonny (bay rum), 1803,
  Savannah Sunrise, Aphrodite, Defiance, Conquest, Flowers in the Dark,
  Blood Oath, Krampus, 13, Lemongrass and Geranium, Rose of Phrygia,
  Pan's Pipe, Runaway, Lime, plus retailer-exclusive Propaganda
  (Shave Revolution; maker's own B&B post). **Sunday Funday
  Extravaganza** (addendum 2026-11-07 [catalog]): Dr. Jon's
  small-batch experimental series — six scents enumerated from the
  dataset (51 product-field rows): Cold Fall Morning (2018–2020),
  Sakura Musk (2018, 2026 — the only one that graduated to a WCS
  collab with a real label; see lineages.md), Burning Bay (2019),
  Eau de Orsini (2019), Melange (2019), Blue ("Sunday Funday
  January 2022: Blue", 2022–2026). "Sunday Funday Extravaganza" is
  a SERIES tag, never part of the scent name — strip it when
  matching. **RazWar** (addendum 2026-11-07 [catalog]): a scent made
  for raz*war, the early razor-by-subscription service (Sharpologist
  2015-10-16); 4 product-field rows, 2016. Sources: theshavingedge.com
  2016/2017 reviews; tailorandbarber.com Vol 2 review; B&B 443385;
  sotd_2016–2026_full.json strings. Confidence: established.

- **Chatillon Lux line structure (2026-10-21).** Three distinct lines:
  (1) **Provisions** — the shaving line (toners, aftershaves, EdTs in
  shaving-adjacent scents; 2015–2020 Maher-made, 2020–2026 DG-made, now
  ended — see lineages.md). (2) **Parfums** — pure-fragrance EdT line
  (La Petite Prairie, Treget, Weinstrasse, Confluence, Rose Santal,
  Lost Valley, Admiral, Envoi, Can't Stand the Rain, Sunrise on
  LaSalle), sold as 2oz/$70 with 2mL samples; as of Oct 2026 four EdTs
  carry "...To Be Discontinued" titles — LEAD: is the whole Parfums
  line migrating to Maher Olfactive? (3) **Side Project Series** —
  limited fragrance releases (Biblio, 3SHEETS, Nefertiti; Biblio made
  CaFleureBon 2019 best-of lists). PLUS the separate
  **Maher Olfactive** line (own site; vetiver/tuberose accord and
  "Red Skies" in the works per Fragrantica 2026). Catalog implication:
  a "Chatillon Lux X" scent name can exist as BOTH a shaving toner/AS
  (Provisions) and an EdT (Parfums) — format disambiguates, never
  assume the form from the scent name alone. [catalog] Established
  (line names from maker's own copy + DFS quotes; Maher Olfactive
  separate site).

- **Grooming Dept base system (2026-10-22).** The "lines" ARE the bases —
  catalog-relevant because posters treat scent×base as the product
  identity (see the base-as-identity backlog item). Community-reported
  bases (B&B GD club threads, DFS discussions — all community-sourced,
  ranked by posters for post-shave): **Vegan** (2016-17 era, "100% palm
  free" tester); **Tallow & Duck Fat** (early Before the Fire era);
  **Mallard** (duck fat); **Janus** (tallow + vegan variants); **Lusso**
  (first seen on Coattails); **Donkey Milk** incl. the "Donkey Combo
  Base" (donkey milk + duck fat + lamb tallow); **Angel** (lamb tallow,
  one last release after the consolidation); **Primus** (dataset
  "primos" = misspelling); **Beef Tallow**; **Fortis** (value line,
  2019–~2020; licensing verdict 2026-10-27, see note below);
  **Nai** ("New Aion Soap", vegan, post-consolidation —
  expansion confirmed 2026-10-23 via B&B GD club #2,082, "Nai Base (New
  Aion Soap)"; community used "Aion/Nai" interchangeably for the
  base);
  **Kairos** (tallow, post-consolidation; **Kairos SE** = **Special
  Edition** — maker-attested, see addendum 2026-11-07 below; lamb
  tallow + emu oil vs standard Kairos beef tallow; community
  distinguishes OG/"non SE" Kairos vs SE, and 2022 vs 2023 SE);
  **Asinus** (unscented final
  DonkeyBase release). ~2020 consolidation kept only Nai / Kairos /
  Mallard (+ Angel + Asinus finals). **Version numbers matter as era
  attributes, not products**: Kairos v3.3 / v4.7 / v4.8 / 5.0, Mallard
  reformulations — a scent in "original Mallard" vs later Mallard is
  the same product line; do NOT split entries by version. Validator
  rule: key GD products as scent + base; version = attribute.
  [catalog] Established (bases list + consolidation + version-era
  practice from B&B/DFS; Kairos SE = Special Edition, Feb 2022
  launch — see addendum below).

  **Kairos SE run 2026-11-07 [catalog]:** RESOLVED — "SE" =
  **Special Edition**, ESTABLISHED from the maker's own store
  (aionskincare.com/products.json, live 2026-11-07): every Kairos SE
  soap page carries "Formula - A special edition based on Kairos"
  (Ousia, Omnia, Pasión, Luxor, Ketoret, Coattails, Ousia II,
  Mumtaz). CORRECTION to the standing entry: SE launched **February
  2022, not 2023** — Pasión created 2022-02-01 (titled "Kairos +"),
  Ousia created 2022-02-22, Omnia 2022-03-07 (both titled "Kairos SE
  ( Lamb Tallow + Emu Oil )"); first dataset attestation 2022-04-18
  (B_S80, Ousia, via the SE product URL); B&B community use by Aug
  2022 ("GD Omnia Kairos SE", 2022-08-24). The Feb 2023 B&B review
  (636699) reviewed the Jan 2023 drop (Luxor etc.), not the launch.
  Formula = lamb tallow + emu oil (maker titles verbatim); standard
  Kairos = beef tallow (+ coconut milk, goat milk). TWO
  maker-published INCI revisions, both lamb+emu at core: (A) 2022
  list — Ousia/Omnia/Pasión/Ousia II (phenoxyethanol preservative;
  hydroxyethylcellulose); (B) Jan 2023 list — Luxor (created
  2023-01-05) + Mumtaz re-release (tagged Jan2023Release):
  preservative swapped to sodium dehydroacetate + honeysuckle
  (Lonicera) extracts, thickener to tara gum, + oat oil,
  polyglyceryl-4 oleate, bis-diglyceryl polyacyladipate-2; no
  phenoxyethanol, no hydroxyethylcellulose. The B&B poster's "2022
  Kairos SE vs 2023 Kairos SE" distinction is therefore REAL — year
  tags are release/revision tags, not just drop dates. **"Kairos +"**
  is a real maker title form: Pasión alone carries "Kairos + Lamb
  Tallow + Emu Oil" (same 2022 INCI list as Ousia) — treat "+" as an
  SE title variant, never a separate base. RETROACTIVE-RETITLE TRAP
  [catalog]: the maker retitled older products to "Kairos SE"
  (L'avventura created 2019-07-18, Coattails Redux 2019-03-28,
  Mumtaz 2020-12-07) — a product's created_at does NOT date its SE
  status, and pre-2022 "Kairos SE"-titled pages prove nothing about
  what shipped then; the Jan 2023 Mumtaz re-release is the SE
  shipment. "Special Edition" ALONE is ambiguous: Ambrosia (2019)
  is tagged/formula'd "Special Edition" on a DIFFERENT beef-tallow +
  duck-fat formula — never read a bare "Special Edition" tag as
  Kairos SE. The live Formulas page
  (groomingdept.com/pages/formulas, read 2026-11-07) no longer
  documents Kairos SE at all (Maxima / Kairos one-liner / Optimus /
  Mallard only) — products.json is the maker record. Dataset scope:
  exactly 3 genuine "Kairos SE" soap-field strings in the whole
  corpus (B_S80 Ousia 2022-04-18; Ali4451 "GD Kairos SE 2023 Luxor"
  2023-02-16; RedMosquitoMM "Ardent - Kairos SE" 2023-12-02) —
  posters mostly write the scent and let the product URL carry the
  base. Wayback CDX was "Temporarily Offline" this run — no snapshot
  check was possible. NEW NARROW LEADS: (a) Coattails Jan-2023 INCI
  (list B presumed, unread); (b) the "Kairos+" community mention
  (B&B 635274: "the Kairos base for Chamomile & Lemon had been
  updated some") — the Chamomile & Lemon product is a PRE-SHAVE, so
  check whether "+" ever labeled a pre-shave formula or is
  soap-only; (c) Mo's own announcement of the SE launch / 2023
  revision (email list or Facebook, Feb 2022 / Jan 2023) — would pin
  the launch moment better than the Feb 1 product-creation date.

  **Fortis note, 2026-10-27 [catalog]:** Fortis = GD's affordable
  value base (5 oz, $15 at launch per B&B GD club p.72 #1,423;
  shorter ingredient list, simpler fragrance-oil scents; Sharpologist
  'rona-savings review gives the full Fortis ingredient list:
  beef tallow, shea, avocado, cow milk, lanolin). Dataset scope is
  THIN — 16 hits total (2019: 2, 2020: 11, 2022: 3), 8 authors —
  scents: Spa, Rainforest, Incense & Oud, Ingress, Boomer, Le Tabac
  (+ generic "GD Fortis Sample" ×5, shaveSymptomatic 2020). The
  Oleo/WCS licensing question is RETIRED (see lineages.md addendum):
  the idea was one poster's hope, Fortis was dropped in the ~2020
  consolidation, and no licensed Fortis product exists — validators
  must keep Fortis scents as GD-made, scent + Fortis-base products,
  and must NOT attribute any WCS/Oleo soap to the Fortis formula.

- **Aion Skincare-era products (post-rename, ~2024+, see lineages.md
  [catalog]).** Skincare line: **Nourishing Balm** (2023 breakout
  product — dataset post-shave fields explode with it; maker DFS post
  links the groomingdept.com product page), **Hydrating Gel**
  (ingredient listing "Grooming Department Aion Skincare Hydrating
  Gel", inkeedecoder, 2023), **Botanical Toner** (dataset 2025–2026).
  **New soap base: Maxima** (ESTABLISHED 2026-10-27, supersedes the
  lead above). The Aion-era tallow base, launched ~Nov 24, 2023
  (Iris as the first release, Black Friday timing — Leisureguy
  Feb 2024: "first released one year ago, on November 24"; DFS
  2023-11-11 already anticipating the "new AION Skincare Maxima
  base" as a refinement of Kairos SE 2023). Formula (Leisureguy,
  quoting the maker list): aloe vera juice, green tea extract,
  illipe/cupuacu/tucuma/murumuru butters, **lamb tallow**, emu oil,
  sacha inchi oil, ceramides (NP/AP/EOP), lanolin — a long
  skincare-heavy list distinct from every earlier GD base.
  At the Black Friday 2024 sale it was the outgoing base:
  Leisureguy reports "the last Maxima soap that will be made
  with tallow (he's switching to a vegan formula for Maxima)"
  alongside one Mallard soap, with all other sale soaps in the
  new Optimus formula. **RESOLVED 2026-10-27 — the promised
  vegan Maxima DID ship, but as ONE product, not a line
  conversion (maker site, live-browser check 2026-10-27):**
  "Aion Skincare Maxima Vegan Unscented Shaving Soap - Tacit"
  (6.5 oz, sold out, $0.00 listing; product image labeled
  "TACIT / MAXIMA - VEGAN"). The standard Maxima line stayed
  tallow — the maker Formulas page describes Maxima as lamb
  tallow + duck fat + emu oil — and the current shaving-soap
  collection (11 products, all sold out, homepage banner
  "Optimus — Evolved formula. Improved performance.") is
  Optimus-only. So the BF 2024 picture resolves three ways,
  not one: tallow Maxima continued as legacy listings, ONE
  vegan Maxima (Tacit, unscented) shipped, and Optimus became
  the house standard. Catalog rule [catalog]: Tacit is its OWN
  scent+base product (vegan Maxima, unscented); never fold it
  into tallow Maxima, and never read "vegan Maxima" as a
  whole-line conversion. Maker-site Maxima scent list
  (all sold out, $29–29.99): Wonderland, Lavender, After The
  Fire, Unum, Iris, KAI, Vetiver, Tobacco Oud, Ténèbres,
  Trésor (+ Tacit vegan unscented) — note "Vetiver" here vs
  dataset "Vetyver" (spelling variant, same scent family).
  Dataset: ZERO "Maxima" soap-field strings through Oct 2026
  (word-boundary scan 2026-10-27; the earlier substring hits
  were prose "maximal"), and ZERO notes combining "Maxima"
  with "vegan"/"Optimus" — posters do not tag Maxima the way
  they tag Kairos/Mallard, so Maxima-era scents will usually
  need dating by release, not by a base tag. RESIDUAL LEADS:
  (a) Tacit's ingredient list (the maker page shown had no
  ingredient list — vegan status rests on title + label image,
  not ingredients); (b) 2019 "Grooming Dept Tacit" soap strings
  (2 hits: MentholMurph 2019-04-09, LOCO_X 2019-06-12, no base
  tag) — is Aion Tacit a carry-forward of that 2019 scent, or a
  name reuse for a new unscented product? Do NOT fold the 2019
  strings into the vegan Maxima Tacit entry until the base is
  pinned. [catalog]
  **RESOLVED 2026-10-31 (Tacit thread run):**
  (b) CARRY-FORWARD, not a name collision. "Tacit" was already
  GD's unscented TALLOW soap name by September 2018 (DFS
  firsthand post 2018-09-09: "Grooming Dept 'Tacit' Unscented
  Tallow Base" — crawl-verbatim quote; DFS text fetch is
  Anubis-blocked, so cite the search-crawl quote + dataset
  corroboration). The 2 dataset 2019 strings (MentholMurph
  2019-04-09, LOCO_X 2019-06-12, both soap field, no base tag)
  = the GD-era tallow Tacit; a full-corpus word-boundary check
  2026-10-31 finds NO other Tacit soap strings (all other
  "tacit" hits are prose: taciturn, Tacitus, La Tacita De Café).
  The 2024 Aion "Maxima Vegan Unscented Shaving Soap - Tacit"

- **[catalog] Declaration Grooming — base eras: Bison / Icarus /
  Milksteak (established 2026-10-24).** Posters routinely tag the base —
  "Declaration Grooming - Chaipre (Milksteak)", "Declaration Grooming
  *Dirtyver* (Icarus)", "L&L Grooming - Darkfall (Bison base)" — and the
  SAME scent exists in multiple bases (dataset: Darkfall in all three;
  Chaipre in Milksteak + Icarus; Unscented in Milksteak + Bison), so
  catalog key for DG soaps = **scent + base**, version = era attribute.
  Era sequence: **Bison** (the original bison-tallow base, continuous
  from L&L Grooming through the Nov 2017 rename to Declaration Grooming —
  one formula, July 2016 – Sept 2019; see continuity addendum below) →
  **Icarus** (~2018; bison tallow + lamb tallow + goat's milk
  + colloidal oatmeal per B&B comparison thread; had its OWN scent
  lineup — "Icarus didn't have the same scents as the bison base" per
  B&B 575718 #20) → **Milksteak** (launched Sept 2019; maker's own FB
  announcement relayed on B&B 574293 #40: "the Icarus and the older
  bison base are now officially dead and are being replaced with this
  new Milksteak base"; "The BASE has changed, not the scents" — B&B
  575718 #15). Milksteak is bison-tallow + dairy: Massacre of the
  Innocents packaging (via EWG) lists Bison Tallow, Yogurt, Buttermilk,
  Egg Whites, Coconut Milk, Goat's Milk — the name reflects the dairy.
  Dataset first-uses match: milksteak 2019 (44), icarus 2018 (94),
  bison 2016+. Validator cautions: (1) "(Bison)" is an ERA tag (the
  pre-Milksteak base), not an ingredient tag — ALL THREE bases contain
  bison tallow, so post-2019 "(Bison)" strings are legacy pucks, not
  evidence of a fourth base; (2) Icarus-era scent lineup was distinct —
  don't assume an Icarus string implies a Milksteak equivalent; (3)
  post-2019 untagged DG strings are almost certainly Milksteak (only
  production base) but per P-11 don't auto-default. Sources: B&B
  575718 (2019-10-22; maker-change relay + base comparison #9),
  B&B 574293 p.2 #40 (2019-09-26; maker's FB Milksteak announcement),
  B&B 547858 (Icarus ingredient recollection), EWG packaging listing
  for DG Massacre of the Innocence, sotd_2016–2026_full.json base-tag
  counts (milksteak 226 / icarus 272 / bison 190 soap hits).
  **ADDENDUM — bison-base continuity at the L&L → DG rename RESOLVED
  2026-10-29 (answers backlog item (3)) [catalog].** VERDICT: the bison
  formula did NOT change at the rename — one continuous bison era from
  its July 2016 launch (L&L) to the Sept 2019 Milksteak deprecation.
  RENAME DATE CORRECTION: the rename was announced **~mid-Nov 2017**,
  not "2018" as this entry previously said — dataset's first
  "Declaration Grooming" strings are 2017-11-20 (jdubba, urfrendlipiro,
  CombatCarl13), already phrased "Declaration Grooming (formerly L&L
  Grooming)". Evidence for formula continuity: (1) the rename landed as
  a pure name change — community adopted the "(formerly L&L Grooming)"
  framing from day one, treating it as the same company; (2) the
  maker himself deprecated "Bison and Icarus" as ONE continuous base in
  the Sept 2019 Milksteak announcement (Sharpologist 2020-03-27 quotes
  the maker's statement: "an incredibly difficult decision to deprecate
  Bison and Icarus"); (3) the bison ingredient list is identical across
  the pre-rename record — B&B 530210 (Optimum Lather table, purchase
  Aug 11, 2017) and the early-2017 B&B 522030 L&L-vs-WK shootout quote
  the same list: Stearic Acid, Water, Potassium Hydroxide, Avocado Oil,
  Vegetable Glycerin, Bison Tallow, Mango Seed Butter, Castor Oil,
  Fragrance, Sodium Hydroxide, Lanolin, Bentonite Clay, Sea Buckthorn /
  White Willow / Grape Seed extracts, Phenoxyethanol, Caprylyl Glycol,
  Sorbic Acid; (4) negative evidence — this community flags every base
  change loudly (Icarus got its own thread B&B 540135 in Mar 2018;
  Milksteak a maker announcement relayed B&B 574293 #40), yet ZERO posts
  anywhere claim a formula change at the rename (negative dataset-note
  scan 2017–2018 + web search 2026-10-29); (5) 2018 threads casually
  treat the bison base as one thing across the name ("I'll just keep
  buying the old bison tallow base" — B&B 556912, Dec 2018; "the old
  bison tallow base that doesn't have EDTA in it" distinguishes it from
  Icarus on preservatives). Catalog: validators may treat ALL bison-era
  DG/L&L soap strings (July 2016 – Sept 2019) as the same base formula;
  "(Bison)" tags on L&L-name and DG-name strings are the same era tag.
  Pre-bison note: L&L launched with a VEGAN formula before the bison
  base ("This is the vegan formula before bison hit" — NoLogonServAvailable,
  2016 dataset note on L&L After the Rain Vegan), so pre-July-2016 L&L
  strings are a DIFFERENT base — do not fold into the bison era.
  Sources: dataset rename mining (sotd_2017_full.json); B&B 530210,
  522030, 540135, 556912, 575718; Sharpologist DG After The Rain review
  (2020-03-27, maker's Milksteak statement); dailylather 2016-11-01
  (bison launch, early July 2016).
  **ADDENDUM — Icarus scent lineup RESOLVED 2026-10-28 (answers backlog
  item (2)) [catalog].** Dataset mining (sotd_2016–2026_full.json, soap
  field, base-tag cross-tab) + B&B release threads give the Icarus-era
  lineup as a CURATED SUBSET of the bison catalog, not a full carryover
  — which is what reconciles the two 2019 B&B claims ("the BASE has
  changed, not the scents" vs "Icarus didn't have the same scents as
  the bison base"): the scents that DID move kept their names across
  bases, but many bison scents never shipped in Icarus at all, and
  several Icarus scents were new. Release order, B&B-dated: **Santal
  Auster** first (Mar 14, 2018, Chatillon Lux Parfums collab, $31/4 oz,
  B&B 540135 — "only available in Santal Auster" at launch; a one-time
  run per B&B 575718 #18) → **Sweet Lemon, Shore, Contemplation**
  (the 2018 sample trio, B&B 545809) → **Chaipre + Scrumtulescent**
  (Dec 19, 2018, B&B 556912; Dirtyver already "no longer available at
  the DG website" that week). Dataset Icarus-tagged first uses add, in
  order: Harmony (Sep 2018), Dirtyver + Darkfall + La Forêt de Liguest
  (Oct 2018), Vide Poche (Aug 2018), Sellout (Dec 2018), Lamplight
  Penance + Chaipre (Jan 2019), Sni Mato (Feb 2019), 88 Chestnut +
  Taxes + Nefertiti + Fourth and Pine (Apr–May 2019), Mayflower +
  Taum Sauk (May–Jul 2019), Admiral (Jul 2019); Weinstrasse and Try
  This Soap 2.0 Icarus strings are Jan 2020 legacy-puck uses, not new
  Icarus production (post-Milksteak-launch tags = legacy stock).
  Icarus-attested scent set (soap-field Icarus tags, 300 hits):
  Santal Auster, Sweet Lemon, Shore, Contemplation, Dirtyver,
  Darkfall, Vide Poche, La Forêt de Liguest, Harmony, Sellout,
  Lamplight Penance, Chaipre, Scrumtulescent, Sni Mato, 88 Chestnut,
  Taxes, Nefertiti, Fourth and Pine, Mayflower, Taum Sauk, Admiral,
  Weinstrasse, Try This Soap 2.0. Positive Milksteak-tag carryovers
  from that set: Darkfall (19), Chaipre (14), Shore (4), Mayflower
  (4), Weinstrasse (1). Bison scents that SKIPPED Icarus (zero
  Icarus tags) but reached Milksteak: Yuzu-Rose-Patchouli (8
  Milksteak tags), Massacre of the Innocents (9), Gratiot League
  Square (5), Cuir et Epices (4). Validator cautions: (a) post-2019
  UNTAGGED DG strings are almost certainly Milksteak per the entry
  above, so absence of a Milksteak TAG does not prove a scent never
  shipped in Milksteak — the lineup above is the Icarus-attested
  set, and only the positive Milksteak tags are carryover evidence;
  (b) two single-hit anomalies are LEADS, not carryovers: one
  Milksteak-tagged Dirtyver (Dganjo, 2020-03-30) and one
  Milksteak-tagged Contemplation (wallygator88, 2023-04-15) — a
  later re-release or a legacy-puck mis-tag, unpinned; (c) two
  apparent Icarus tags are CONTAMINATION, not lineup evidence: the
  single "Marshlands | ... Icarus was vast progress" string (note
  text inside the soap field) and the strikethrough "~~Icarus
  Mayflower~~ Bison Cuir et Epices" string (a correction, not an
  Icarus Cuir); (d) Dganjo is the dominant Icarus chronicler — many
  first uses are single-author, so treat first-use dates as
  earliest-observed, not release dates, except where B&B dates them.
  Sources: dataset cross-tab above; B&B 540135 (2018-03-14),
  545809 (samples), 556912 (2018-12-19), 575718 #15/#18/#20
  (2019-10-22).
- **[catalog] DG addendum, 2026-11-07 — the two Milksteak singletons
  RESOLVED as carryovers.** Both single-hit anomalies from the
  2026-10-28 addendum graduate to positive Milksteak carryovers.
  (1) Dirtyver: Dganjo's single "Declaration Grooming Milksteak -
  Dirtyver" (2020-03-30) sits inside a March–May 2020 restock
  cluster of 10 Milksteak tags — Darkfall 2020-03-11, Massacre of the
  Innocents 2020-03-16, Unscented 2020-03-26, Cuir et Epices
  2020-03-29, Dirtyver 2020-03-30, Cuir 2020-03-31 + 04-23 + 05-03,
  Unscented 2020-04-11, Darkfall 2020-05-04 — from an author who in
  the same year tags Icarus legacy pucks (Contemplation, Vide Poche,
  Chaipre, Taum Sauk) and Bison legacy pucks (After the Rain, Cuir,
  Unconditional Surrender) correctly. The Cuir precedent closes the
  case: B&B 575718 #17 (2019-10-22) said Cuir et Epices was "none of
  which have been released in Milksteak yet", yet Dganjo's 4x
  Milksteak Cuir tags (Mar–May 2020) show it reached Milksteak in
  that wave — the rollout was gradual and unannounced, and Dirtyver
  rides it. His Dirtyver uses run TTS-form 2020-01-01 → 2020-03-01,
  the Milksteak tag 2020-03-30, TTS-form again 2020-05-18 →
  2020-08-30 (the TTS link is his default paste; the base tag appears
  when he consciously notes it). Corroborating color: w3strnwrld's
  "Declaration Grooming Dirtyver (the new one)" (2021-11-30).
  (2) Contemplation: wallygator88's single "Declaration Grooming
  Contemplation Milksteak" (2023-04-15) is use #21 of one continuous
  tub: 44 uses 2021–2025 — 17 TTS-form (2021) + 1 (2022) + the
  Milksteak tag + 2 bare (2023-04-28/29) + numbered uses (22)
  2023-05-10 → (44) in 2025. He tags bases elsewhere (After the Rain
  Bison x8, 2019–2020) and never tags this tub Icarus; first use 2021
  = Milksteak era. (3) Maker-side Wayback CDX check (2026-11-07,
  declarationgrooming.com/collections/shaving-soaps/products/*,
  2019–2022, statuscode:200, collapse=urlkey): 65 distinct product
  URLs, NOT truncated. Both scents appear ONLY as Icarus-slug pages
  — Dirtyver `dirtyver-shaving-soap-icarus-base-4oz-autumn-seasonal`
  (2019-11-20) and Contemplation
  `contemplation-shaving-soap-icarus-base-4oz` (2021-11-08); no
  Milksteak-slug page for either. This is NEUTRAL, not negative: the
  same list shows the maker kept the original slug through base
  changes — Darkfall (19 dataset Milksteak tags), Chaipre (14), Shore
  (4), Mayflower (4) and Weinstrasse (1) all ship Milksteak on
  Icarus/bison slugs, and After the Rain kept its bison slug
  (2021-03-22) despite the Milksteak review (Sharpologist, March
  2020). A Milksteak slug exists only for scents that never had an
  Icarus page (Massacre, Tribute, B-Cubed, Dayman, Nightman,
  Opulence, etc.) or new products — Chaipre 2.0 is a distinct
  Milksteak product (slug `chaipre-2-0-shaving-soap-milksteak-base-4oz`,
  2022-08-13). RULE: never read a DG product's base from its slug
  alone; the slug dates the original listing. The CDX inventory also
  confirms products.json has zero captures (2019–2022) and that
  Sellout only ever had an Icarus page (2020-11-12). Full 65-row list
  saved in goal hidden_files
  (dg_cdx_shaving_soaps_2026-11-07.txt). (4) Catalog guidance: fold
  "Declaration Grooming Milksteak - Dirtyver" and "Declaration
  Grooming Contemplation Milksteak" to the Milksteak-variant entries
  of Dirtyver and Contemplation, never to the Icarus clusters.
  Residual caveat: no maker listing names a Milksteak Dirtyver/
  Contemplation page, and each scent still has a single author — a
  second author's Milksteak tag or a tub-label photo would firm both.
  Confidence: established at dataset level (author-reliability +
  cluster/sequence structure + Cuir precedent); maker-side
  confirmation still wanted.
  Sources: dataset cross-tab (sotd_2016–2026_full.json, 2026-11-07);
  B&B 575718 #17 (2019-10-22); Wayback CDX, declarationgrooming.com
  shaving-soaps products, 2019–2022 (65 rows).

- **[catalog] DG rename-era loose ends RESOLVED 2026-11-08 (answers
  backlog items (b) + (d)).**
  (d) **"DFS 2017" is NOT an event or meetup — it is a limited-edition
  L&L Grooming soap + aftershave made for the Damn Fine Shave forum.**
  First dataset use 2017-06-08 (120inna55, notes-field "L&L Grooming
  - DFS 2017 Limited Edition"); TTS collection 737; soap + splash.
  Full-corpus census (sotd_2016–2026_full.json): **125 rows / 22
  authors, 2017-06-08 → 2026-10-26** (2017: 45, 2018: 39, then a
  destash tail; top authors Ythin 32, viceayala 31, 35048467 18).
  Scent notes, firsthand: **sandalwood (main note) + blood orange +
  amber** — viceayala 2017-08-03 ("Sandalwood was always the main
  note, but it pairs so nice with Blood Orange"), sgrdddy
  2020-03-11 ("(L&L Grooming Bison Tallow - DFS 2017) Them:
  Sandalwood, Blood Orange, Amber"), 97runner 2018-06-19 ("the
  citrus smell of DFS"), Ythin 2017-10-02 ("a darker citrus").
  Base: **BISON tallow** — Spankmeister88 2018-11-15 ("Performance
  was excellent as is usual for the L&L/Declaration Bison base"),
  Ythin 2018-10-24 ("Bison is a great base"), sgrdddy's own
  "Bison Tallow" tag. **CORRECTION to the backlog premise:** the
  rename did NOT debut at a "DFS 2017" event — the first
  "Declaration Grooming (formerly L&L Grooming)" strings
  (2017-11-20, urfrendlipiro) are DFS 2017 soap strings because
  TryThatSoap had renamed the brand in its collection titles; the
  rename's own announcement channel is still unpinned (the maker's

  and Mann made a SEPARATE "DFS 2017 LE" the same year (BourbonInExile

  shops in the Appalachian mountains") — never fold L&L DFS 2017

  `unverified/soap/barrister-amp-mann-dfs-2017-soap`).
  Catalog: `unverified/soap/l-l-dfs-2017` is promotion-ready
  (scent notes + base + launch + 125 rows / 22 authors on file);
  the aftershave clusters (`l-l-dfs-2017`, `l-l-dfs-2017-le`,

  above stands on its own).
  (b) **Pre-bison L&L vegan formula — DISCONTINUED at the bison
  launch, never run in parallel.** Bison retail launch = early–mid
  July 2016: first dataset bison strings 2016-07-08–12 (twagshot
  2016-07-12 "my new arrival: L&L Marshlands Bison Tallow"; beslayed
  2016-07-16 "Bison Kidney fat edition"; Quadricwan 2016-07-20

- **[catalog] Barrister and Mann — base eras as identity (established
  2026-10-24).** Posters routinely tag the base — "B&M Seville
  (Excelsior)", "B&M Leviathan (Glissant)", "B&M Leviathan (Omnibus)",
  "B&M Reserve Spice (omnibus)" — and the same scent spans bases
  (Seville in Reserve, Glissant, Excelsior, Omnibus; one poster owned
  "42 in every base"), so catalog key for B&M soaps = **scent +
  base**, version = era attribute. Era sequence (maker history via
  nebrito brand story + B&B announcement threads): **White Label +
  Black Label** (original lines) → **Glissant** (2015; slicker) →
  **Excelsior** (Nov 2018; dev code "Purple Possum"; Vespers was the
  launch scent; "a mix of the formula Glissante and the Reserve",
  LANOLIN-bearing; maker's Excelsior squiggle symbol on all Excelsior
  soaps except Vespers) → **Omnibus** (debut 2021-09-28 with Leviathan;
  replaced Reserve + Excelsior + Soft Heart; lanolin-free). Parallel
  budget/vegan lines: **Latha** (lanolin-free, ~2015–2018; 790 dataset
  soap hits — its own scent lineup: Oceana, Taiga, Sandalwood etc.),
  **Tre Citta** (vegan base, 2016–2018 per maker: developed alongside

Lid also carries "BARRISTER AND MANN", "DICKENS / SHAVING SOAP", "Net Wt. 4 oz", www.barristerandmann.com, barcode "8 57483 00529 5". No base name ("Glissant"/"White Label") is printed on the lid — the verdict comes from the INCI comparison, not a base name. **Catalog guidance [catalog]: validators MAY tag Nov/Dec 2015 Dickens strings as White Label; NEVER as Glissant.** The B&B 483633 Mar 2016 "ridiculously slick" post stands as a performance impression only, not base evidence. **Caveats (recorded, not verdict-breaking):** (1) the final lid ingredient reads only "[obscured] Acetate" — "Tocopherol" is plausible but NOT legible and is not asserted; (2) "Silk Protein" (present in the page INCI between Allantoin and Tocopherol Acetate) was NOT seen on the lid — the read states only one word is obscured, so this is a one-item lid-vs-page discrepancy, either a missed word at the rim or a lid-print variant; it touches no Glissant discriminator → (c4-narrow-108); (3) the tub bottom was not visible (tub sits in a wooden bowl) — clear-bottom vs white-bottom unconfirmed; only the pale frosted/translucent side-wall band was observed; (4) the 2015 provenance rests on the poster's own caption "Dickens (2015)".

  **CORRECTION 2026-11-12 ~15:06 CST — Figarose was never delisted [catalog]:** the 03:06 "delisted" verdict above is RETRACTED (same correction already recorded in hardware_lore.md from the 05:06 live-browser read). Naked Armor products.json read live this run (250 products): "Barrister and Mann Figarose Aftershave Splash", handle `barrister-and-mann-figarose-aftershave-splash`, available:true, $27.00; "Barrister and Mann Figarose Shaving Soap (Omnibus Base)", handle `barrister-and-mann-figarose-shaving-soap-omnibus-base`, available:false, $27.00. Both products survive under brand-prefixed handles; the soap is out of stock, not delisted. NA remains a live evidence route for both (the .js-404 + page-404 pair was the old short handle redirecting, per the redirect caveat in the method notes).

  Cool 2023-04-06; Cologne Russe 2023-04-06; Fougère Classique,
  Lavender, and The Full Measure of Man 2023-04-10 (all sold out
  now). CAVEAT: created_at dates the retailer listing, and the
  Apr-2023 cluster is a bulk import — read it as "listed by Apr
  2023", never as a launch date. Dataset Omnibus tags prove earlier
  circulation: Lavender Omnibus in posters' hands Jan 2022
  (Sp00kyP00tis ×3, "Reserve Lavender (Omnibus)"), Cool Omnibus Jul
  2022 (Madflava81, trythatsoap "(Omnibus)" link; "Reserve Cool
  (omnibus)" Feb–Nov 2023). Omnibus chapter: base launch Dec 2021
  → trim Feb 2024. The Omnibus reissue dropped "Reserve" from the
  title — Naked Armor's Cool page is the evidence ("Omnibus reissue
  of Barrister's Reserve Cool; 'Reserve' dropped from the reissue
  title", cited on verified entry soap/barrister-and-mann-cool).
  **Maker store, live 2026-11-10 ~19:09 CST (85 products):** NO

  kind; NO Full Measure shaving product (only "Hand & Body Soap:
  The Full Measure of Man", created 2021-02-12, sold out); Lavender

  "Lavender, Interrupted" EdP (sold out) is a DIFFERENT product.
  METHOD NOTE: most core shaving soaps show available:false on this
  read (Seville, Waves, Spice, Cheshire...) — the store is between

- **[catalog] B&M "Behold the Whatsis!" — release arc (established

  (grapefruit, rose, tangerines, sugar cookies, dryer-warm blankets;

  Initiative), released THREE times, never a standing line:

  (B&B 558477, quoted verbatim: March 2nd release list; the
  announcement's "Dr. Seuss's 104th birthday" is the maker's own

  date); first dataset use 2019-03-12 (TheBeerMoose, "dialing in
  the new base"); 13 Excelsior-tagged soap rows, all 2019 (incl.
  MalthusTheShaver ×5). **(2) Apr 2022, Omnibus base** — Daily
  Lather history table (updated 2025-05-05): "Mar 2019 April 2022";
  firsthand re-release narration, Madflava81 2022-04-16 ("I'm glad
  this soap is re-released, and I already have it on the way in the
  mail"); firsthand Omnibus attestation, unitedstatesghost Dec 1–10,
  2022 ("First shave with this scent as well as omnibus"); the
  Naked Armor listing copy is the 2022 revival copy ("for what
  would have been Dr. Seuss's 118th birthday" = Mar 2, 2022) and
  its ingredient list is the Omnibus INCI verbatim (cupuaçu, goat
  milk powder, marshmallow root, slippery elm — all absent from
  Excelsior; Daily Lather Omnibus list). CAUTION: one conflicting
  tag — Madflava81's 2022-05-09 "(Excelsior)" — is most plausibly
  his 2019 tub (his Apr 16 note says the re-release was still in
  the mail), not evidence the 2022 batch was Excelsior. **(3)
  Mar 2, 2025, "Reborn" — Lockhart's Authentic exclusive** (Behind
  the Brew podcast, Apr 16, 2025 episode: "Behold The Whatsis
  Reborn will drop on March 2nd 2025 and is available only at the
  Lockhart's Authentic shop"); bundle soap + aftershave, $42.90,
  listing created 2026-10-29 (re-listing), available:true
  2026-11-10; copy names kokum/shea/coconut milk/goat milk =
  Omnibus-era base (lead-grade, no full INCI read). One dataset
  row (BostonPhotoTourist 2025-03-24, "Barrister and Mann/Lockhart's
  Grooming Behold the Whatsis! Reborn"). **Dataset census
  (241,070 rows):** 270 soap-field rows / 82 authors, 180
  aftershave-field, 3 fragrance-field, 67 notes-only; years 2019
  (85) → 2020 (28) → 2021 (14) → 2022 (48, Apr–May cluster =
  re-release) → 2023 (73) → 2024 (10) → 2025 (6) → 2026 (6);
  last uses Oct 2026 (destash tail). **The 2023 Q1 cluster (44
  rows Jan–Mar) is author-driven, NOT a re-release:** souleater7173
  was killing a tub ("Decided I want to kill a tub before I'm
  allowed to buy any more soap", 2023-01-13) + Madflava81; no

  cite a 2023 re-release. **Retailer-listing caveat:** the Naked
  Armor Whatsis soap listing (created 2023-04-06, in the Apr 3–10
  Omnibus bulk batch; sold out, $22.00) is a listing migration,
  not a launch — same rule as the five trimmed scents. **Catalog
  status [catalog]:** verified soap
  (`soap/barrister-and-mann-behold-the-whatsis`, NA listing
  evidence), aftershave + fragrance via set cross-validation —
  but the **fragrance entry is a FLAG, not a fact**: its
  attestation is exactly 2 uses / 1 author (unitedstatesghost,

  FRAGRANCE field while his notes describe shaving with the

- **[catalog] B&M Latha addendum (established 2026-10-28; corrects
  and completes the entry above).** Latha is a lanolin-free TALLOW
  base — the B&B Oceana-thread claim that Latha was "the non-tallow
  line" is WRONG (corrected on the same thread: the non-tallow base
  was Tre Citta; Latha's ingredient list carries potassium/sodium
  tallowate, argan oil + shea butter superfats, sodium citrate,
  sodium lactate, and NO lanolin, coconut milk, or silk — all three
  of which Glissant has). Launched ~July 2015 (Limon and Oceana both
  dated Jul 2015 in the Daily Lather history table); discontinued
  by maker announcement **February 15, 2018** ("the end of a two and
  a half year run"; reason: fine-fragrance direction + unsustainable
  production; retailers stocked up and final stock sold through).
  All post-Feb-2018 dataset uses are destash/remainder — the 2020
  bump (158 soap hits) is two heavy users (BourbonInExile 66,
  Lambaar12 28), not a relaunch; the tail runs through Oct 2026.
  Canonical lineup (Daily Lather Latha table, 10 scents, each with
  a matching alcohol-free aftershave): **Latha** (classic barbershop:
  lavender, vanilla, oakmoss, carnation, geranium, heliotrope,
  musk), **Lavanda**, **Le Petit Chypré** (coumarin chypre; splash
  mentholated), **Limon** (Jul 2015; orange/lemon/rose), **Oceana**
  (Jul 2015; aquatic/bergamot/jasmine/sandalwood), **Sandalwood**
  (black currant/sandalwood/amber), **Taiga** (pine/balsam/cedar),
  **Figgy Puddin'** (seasonal; green fig/oak/cinnamon/clove/berry),
  **Unscented**, **Velvet** (for women: violet/iris/cherry/praline).
  Dataset (soap-field substring over sotd_2016–2026 yearly files:
  872 hits, 179 authors; the entry above's "790" was a narrower
  scope): Taiga 145/41 authors, Limon 132/28, Oceana 90/48,
  Sandalwood 78/30, Lavanda 75/26, Unscented 52/7, "Latha Original"
  49/19, Le Petit Chypre 36 (+14 "Le Petite" misspellings, 11 "LPC"),
  Figgy Puddin(g) ~25, Velvet 16/5. CORE-LINE OVERLAP (scent+base
  identity consequence): **Taiga** returned in Soft Heart (45 SH
  strings from Dec 2019) and as a core Winter scent Dec 2021
  ("originally created as a member of our Latha line");
  **Oceana** returned in Soft Heart (~2021; 25 SH strings
  2021-03-31–2024-07-31) and as a core Summer scent Jul 2023;
  **Le Petit Chypre** returned in Soft Heart (Dec 2019 strings).
  Limon, Lavanda, Figgy Puddin', Velvet, and barbershop Latha show
  NO core return in the Daily Lather table. Validator cautions:
  (1) **Sandalwood name reuse — do NOT fold**: the 2021 Soft Heart
  Sandalwood (180 SH strings from 2020-12-05; Apr 2022 Limited
  Edition entry) claims a DIFFERENT origin (accord developed for
  the Fougère Gothique EdP) and different character ("most
  natural-smelling sandalwood") from Latha Sandalwood
  (black currant twist) — same name, separate scent+base products;
  (2) **"Latha Original" = the barbershop Latha scent**, not
  Unscented (Unscented is separately tagged; "Original" is
  posters' disambiguator for the line's namesake scent);
  (3) **"Latha Lavender" (7 soap hits: ChrisVanMeer 2022,
  Ramjet615 2023–2025 + AS) = Lavanda mislabel** — no Lavender
  scent exists in the Latha table, and Reserve Lavender is a
  different line; fold into Lavanda. Other spellings: Lavenda /
  Lavada, Le Petite Chypre, Tiaga, Sandlewood. (4) **"Latha
  Osmanthus" is NOT a product** — frankieIVfingers' DIY
  (2018 "B&M x Me Latha Osmanthus"; 2020 posts explicit: "Latha
  Unscented + Osmanthus Absolute" / "with Osmanthus Absolute");
  Rdthedo/wallygator88 2020 strings reference his creation. Fold
  into Latha Unscented with a mod note, never a new entry.
  (5) **Velvet strings never carry "Latha"** ("Barrister and Mann
  Velvet", 16 hits) — matcher must still map them to Latha Velvet;
  no core-line Velvet exists. LEAD only: "Latha was the test bench
  for Glissant" (B&B Oceana thread #7, single forum claim) —
  chronology is thin (Latha Jul 2015 vs Glissant's Nov 2015 Le
  Grand Chypre debut) and the formulas differ on lanolin/coconut
  milk/silk; do not cite as lineage. Sources: dailylather.com
  History of Barrister and Mann (updated May 5, 2025; Latha section
  + ingredient lists + standard-table return entries); B&B 577827
  (Oceana thread, incl. the non-tallow correction + Soft Heart
  Maggard description); B&B 595249 (Oceana Soft Heart return);
  dataset counts above.

## B&M Soft Heart release list run, 2026-10-30

- **[catalog] B&M Soft Heart release list (established 2026-10-30;
  RESOLVES the backlog's "B&M Soft Heart release list" item).**
  PP8 = Soft Heart, confirmed as the internal/test name: the PP8 test
  batch circulated among 4 dataset posters Sep 16, 2018 – Apr 16, 2019
  ("PP8 tester" / "PP8 test batch", 15 strings) during Excelsior
  development, and poster strings tie PP8 to the released base
  directly ("B&M Big Leap Soft Heart (aka PP8)"; "Barrister and Mann
  - Paganini's Violin (Soft Heart Series) in PP-8"; "B&M Hallows
  PP-8"; "Barrister & Mann Sandalwood PP8"; "Barrister and Mann
  PP8 Oceana"). The public "Soft Hearts" series launched Dec 2019 —
  ZERO Soft-Heart-tagged soap strings exist before 2019-12-05 (468
  hits, 180 distinct strings, sotd_2016–2026 soap fields).
  ERA ARC: (1) Sep 2018–Apr 2019: PP8 test batch; testers preferred it
  but Excelsior (harder, longer-lasting) won the house-base slot
  (B&B 613527 quoting Will's video). (2) Dec 2019–Sep 2021: limited
  "Soft Hearts" series — mechanism per B&B 594535 p.2 (Sep 2020):
  "the occasional release of old discontinued [scents]" plus
  seasonals and Unscented. (3) Sep 2021: Excelsior retired; Will
  confirmed on Facebook that all non-Reserve production moves to an
  updated (hardened) SoftHeart base (B&B 613527). (4) Sep 28, 2021:
  Omnibus debuts with Leviathan and replaces Reserve + Excelsior +
  Soft Heart (maker's own statement via B&B 614345) — a weeks-long
  modified-Soft-Heart standard window. (5) Post-2021 SH strings are
  destash of phase-2 tubs (every post-2021 string is a phase-2 scent).
  RELEASE LIST (dataset positive base-tags; external corroboration
  where noted): **Taiga** (Dec 2019 launch, TTS product_type=soap_le;
  Latha-scent revival), **Le Petit Chypre** (Dec 2019, TTS soap_le;
  Latha-scent revival), **Amazelnut** (Jan 2020; Daily Lather: "one of
  our favorite releases from the Soft Heart series" + Nov 2021 Winter
  return), **Big Leap** (Mar 2020 Spring), **Unscented** (Apr 2020+,
  48 hits / 8 authors), **Diamond** (May 2020; BEER soap — poster
  notes "the beer added in Diamond makes the base even softer"),
  **Paganini's Violin** (Aug–Oct 2020), **Ferox** (Sep–Oct 2020),
  **Hallows** (Oct 2020 Fall seasonal — B&B 594535 p.2 pre-order
  note), **Sandalwood** (Dec 2020 SH-series LE, the Fougère-Gothique-
  EdP sandalwood accord — NOT Latha Sandalwood; returned Apr 2022 LE
  — Daily Lather), **Brew Ha-Ha** (Dec 2020), **Roam** (Dec 2020
  Christmas release — B&B 600350 review of "SoftHeart Roam"; OG Roam
  scent, not Roam II), **Rhapsody** (Feb/Mar 2021 Spring), **Oceana**
  (Mar/Apr 2021 seasonal — Maggard product URL
  "...-oceana-artisan-shaving-soap-4oz-soft-heart-base-seasonal";
  Latha-scent revival), **Figarose** (Sep 2021+, 11 hits / 4
  authors — straddles the modified-SH standard window).
  SINGLE-AUTHOR LEADS (positive tags, treat as unconfirmed releases):
  **Petrichor** (Dec 2021–Jan 2022, MalthusTheShaver ×2; the two
  2021-06-17 "petrichor" hits are Roam notes containing the word,
  not the scent), **42** (2022–2023, sahenders ×2 — could be the
  Sep 2021 modified-SH window, since 42 was in production then),
  **Cool** (2025, Ramjet615 ×3 — the same author who mislabeled
  Lavanda as "Latha Lavender"; possible mis-tag, never cite).
  VALIDATOR CAUTIONS: (1) "PP8" strings = Soft Heart; "PP8 tester" /
  "PP8 test batch" = the 2018–2019 test era, never a product. (2)
  The Excelsior squiggle ALSO appears on Soft Heart (already
  recorded, B&B 614345 p.50) — with this list, squiggle + Taiga /
  Hallows / Rhapsody / etc. = Soft Heart, resolving the symbol's
  ambiguity for these scents. (3) Post-2021 untagged strings for
  these scents are NOT SH by default (P-11; Omnibus replaced SH).
  (4) TTS product_type=soap_le on the Dec 2019 Taiga/LPC strings =
  Limited Edition confirmation for the launch. Sources: dataset

## Long Rifle run, 2026-10-23

- **Long Rifle "Black Label" line/formula (established 2026-10-23).**
  As of the maker's Jan-2025 "Big Changes" announcement, ALL Long
  Rifle shaving soaps were reformulated to the **Black Label wool-fat
  formula** (lanolin-forward; maker: "Shavers deserve the thick lather
  provided by lanolin"), and ALL shave tonics to the Black Label
  tonic formula (botanicals + jojoba). Pre-2025 base was locally-
  sourced beef suet tallow (maker's 2020 Earth Day post); June 2025
  dataset ingredient lists for Hivernant still show tallow = the
  transition was in progress through 2025. [catalog]: base = era
  attribute on Long Rifle entries (pre-2025 tallow era vs 2025+
  Black Label wool-fat era); "Black Label" is the formula name, not
  a sub-brand. Candles' rename partner **Big White Yeti** (candle
  maker) noted for lineages.

## Arko run, 2026-10-26

- **[catalog] Arko Men After Shave Cream (50 ml) — pre-2015 line vs
  2015 relaunch (established 2026-10-26).** Arko is Evyap's men's
  brand (Turkey; Evyap also makes Duru, per B&B 422929). The US
  distributor catalog (Kev and Cooper, Jan 2016) states the whole
  Arko Men line was **relaunched in 2015 with a new formula and
  design**: After Shave Cream 50 ml in **4 varieties — Cool,
  Comfort, Fresh, Sensitive**; After Shave Balm 150 ml in the same
  4; After Shave Cologne 250 ml in 5 (Cool, Comfort, Fresh,
  Platinum Protection, Gold Power). The pre-2015 naming was
  messier (B&B 422929, Sept 2014): creams described as Cool, Extra
  Sensitive, Maximum Comfort; balms/colognes as Adventure, Aqua,
  Ice Mint. Dataset attestations of the older names: "Arko Aqua
  balm" / "Arko Balm Aqua" (2016), "Arko Ice Mint Balm" (2017),
  "Arko Classic Aftershave Cream" (2017), "Arko Adventure splash"
  (2020), and if0rg0t2remember's "Arko Citrus After Shave Cream"
  (2021-01-25).
  - **Date correction to the dataset note:** if0rg0t2remember
    dates his hoarded tubes' discontinuation "some time around
    2013". The documented change is the 2015 relaunch replacing
    the old formula/packaging — his "~2013" is approximate at
    best, and may date the ShoeBox clearance sale rather than
    Arko's change. Do NOT cite 2013 as the old line's end date;
    bracket it as "pre-2015 line, replaced 2015". It was also NOT
    a full discontinuation: the four core names (Cool/Comfort/
    Fresh/Sensitive) continue in the new packaging — a B&B
    "sad to see go" poster (Dec 2021) puts it the same way:
    "They still make like one or two scents but it's in a new
    packaging, costs more, and I have no idea if it's the same
    formula. I still have four tubes of the old scents."
  - **Catalog guidance [catalog]:** (1) Cool/Comfort/Fresh/
    Sensitive across the 2015 boundary = ONE product each, relaunch
    = era attribute, never a product split; (2) Adventure, Aqua,
    Ice Mint, Classic, Action, Citrus are pre-relaunch names —
    keep them as their own entries, do NOT fold them into Cool/
    Comfort/Fresh/Sensitive without evidence; (3) "Arko Citrus"
    is single-author and unpinned — it may be a scent name or a
    descriptor (Adventure was described by a B&B poster as a
    strong artificial citrus) — do NOT alias Citrus → Adventure;
    (4) "Arko Cool Cream" / "Arko Comfort Cream" are AMBIGUOUS
    between the After Shave Cream and the Shaving Cream tubes
    (both exist in Cool/Comfort) — field and poster context
    decide, never auto-apply; (5) the 2022 dataset spike (41
    "Arko Comfort" aftershave hits) is TWO authors, not a
    revival — one heavy user, not market activity.
  Sources: Kev and Cooper Arko catalog (Jan 2016, distributor);
  B&B 422929 (Sept 2014); B&B 617846 p.2 #36 (Dec 2021);
  sotd_2016–2026_full.json (Arko aftershave-field counts +
  if0rg0t2remember 2021-01-25 note). Confidence: 2015 relaunch
  + 4-variety lineup established (distributor catalog); old-name
  list established (B&B + dataset); Citrus identity = lead.

- **[catalog] Asylum Shave Works — "English Collection" shaving
  creams (established 2026-10-27).** A CREAM line inside the
  BullGoose house brand, distinct from the Asylum tallow soaps
  (Colonia / Flying Mango / Frankincense & Myrrh / Old Virginia /

  soap — do NOT fold cream strings into any soap entry, and do
  NOT read "English Collection" as a maker (it is the line name;
  brand of record stays Asylum Shave Works, sold via BullGoose).
  Established lineup (FIVE scents, confirmed 2026-11-06 from the
  BullGoose collection page, Wayback capture 2022-01-17):
  **Charcoal, European Barbershop, Bay Rum, Aloe, Bergamot** —
  full product title form: "Asylum English Collection Shaving
  Cream (<scent>)" (170g). The first three were already in
  BullGoose's bestseller list as quoted by Sharpologist; Aloe
  and Bergamot are new from the collection page (Aloe captured
  2022-01-17, Bergamot 2022-01-17). The line
  was already a BullGoose bestseller by Sharpologist's article
  published 2020-11-13 — so the line predates Nov 2020; launch
  date itself is unpinned (lead).
  Dataset presence is thin but real: exactly ONE full-form string
  in 2016–2026, KerblimeySkal 2024-01-27, "Sensitive Skin |
  Asylum English Collection Shaving Cream - Charcoal", plus the
  same author's shortened 2024-06-12 "Asylum Shave Works -
  Charcoal" (same cream, almost certainly — fold the shortened
  form into the Charcoal cream entry, not into a soap). PARSER
  CAUTION [catalog]: the leading "Sensitive Skin |" is
  KerblimeySkal's own skin-type prefix, NOT part of the product
  name — he prefixes the same string to unrelated soaps
  (Moonsoaps Havana, Amaretto Speciale, Spearhead Seaforth!,
  Macduff's Santa's Cheer, Nov 2023–Jan 2024); strip author
  skin-type prefixes before matching. The 2024 poster's note is
  firsthand color only: bought "their branded soap" from
  BullGoose (he had just learned BullGoose was local to him)
  and found it "kind of burn-y" — NOT evidence the cream is
  marketed for sensitive skin, and not a formula claim.
  Contract maker UNPINNED (lead): unlike Levante (Nice Pack,
  Spain) and the tallow soaps (SV / WSP), no source yet names
  who makes the English Collection creams, and "English" in the
  line name is NOT evidence of English manufacture — do not

  collection page (2022-01-17). Confidence: line existence +
  five scents + BullGoose house-brand channel established;

  2022-01-17 lists all five creams (170g); individual product

- **[catalog] Envy Shave — the "Envy White" knot-maker brand (established
  2026-11-01).** Envy Shave was a mid-2010s artisan that sold KNOTS,

  Soap line (few strings): Senbò, Winter Forage, Sakura Sen
  (envyshave.com product URL attested in a dataset string).

  61 authors — classic 2016-17 artisan boom then legacy/NOS tail.
  Brand status: QUIET WIND-DOWN — maker site dead (envyshave.com

  closure announcement found; current-soap evidence nil. Evidence
  route = forums/photos/dataset, never the live maker site.

  the knot brand, not to Rudy Vey / DG / C&R as makers. LEADS:

## Talent Soap Factory base timeline run, 2026-11-04
**[catalog]** TSF = Talent Soap Factory (Etsy artisan, maker Aaron; see
lineages.md). The vegan line iterates roughly once a year (RedMosquitoMM
2025-03-11 firsthand: "TSF iterates on their soap base regularly — about
once a year as far as I can tell"), so base = an era attribute (P-03/P-17),
never a product split:
- **Original formula** (2021–2022; worbx firsthand).
- **Formula 9 / Formula 10** (2022; worbx firsthand; "original formula"
  took more water than F9/F10 — F10 less water-sensitive).
- **Kokum-butter vegan** (2023; RedMosquitoMM 2023-03-18: "kokum butter
  heavy vegan bases that can take a deluge of water").
- **"Matilda" base** (by Dec 2023; PhilosphicalZombie 2023-12-20 notes
  "an updated 'Matilda' base" — name attested, exact launch unpinned).
- **"Tradditional Vegan"** (Feb 2024; RedMosquitoMM 2024-02-20: switched
  to it recently from a "Cocoa Butter" base; harsher on skin, slicker).
- **Sweet-almond-oil vegan** (pre-mango; snoo-ting 2025-06-24: "previous
  vegan bases that includes sweet almond oil... performs a lot better
  than their more recent mango butter base").
- **Mango-butter vegan** (current vegan, 2025–2026; maker-attested
  ingredient list on the Etsy listing: Potassium Stearate, Glycerin,
  Orbignya Oleifera (Babassu) Seed Oil, Mangifera Indica (Mango) Seed
  Butter, Sodium Stearate, Aqua, Persea Gratissima (Avocado) Oil,
  Parfum, Ricinus Communis (Castor) Seed Oil). Breadheater9876
  2025-06-17 quotes the same list ("Tea Shoppe is labeled as
  traditional vegan shaving soap").
- **"Betti Base"** (Nov 2024; PhilosphicalZombie 2024-11-23: "TSF's
  newer Betti Base" — placement vs mango-butter unpinned).
- **Duck-fat base** (NEW, launched ~Nov 2025 — first dataset uses
  snoo-ting 2025-11-13/14 + B_S80 2025-11-01; "new TSF duck fat base",
  "better than their mango butter base"; 5 "Duck Fat Shave Soap"
  listings on Etsy 2026-11-04; catalog entry
  `unverified/soap/talent-soap-factiry-2nd-wind-duck-fat` — note the
  typo'd brand slug "factiry"; see lineages.md).
Validator note: the yearly churn means a TSF scent released 2022 vs
2025 is very likely in a different base even when posters don't tag
it — never carry a base attribute across years. Community standing:
the vegan line is highly regarded (Lather Games 2024 sponsor,
MM2025 final-four "TSF domination era"; PhilosphicalZombie: "I have
eight of their soaps! I wouldn't have kept buying them if they
weren't good").

## The Shave Mercantile house line — "The Simple Life" + 7 scents, one vegan base, Music City Suds-made (established 2026-11-07) [catalog]
- **Line name:** "The Simple Life" is the house line's name per the maker (Unscented page: "\"The Simple Life\" is The Shave Mercantile's trio of house brand shaving soaps beginning with an Unscented soap"). The trio's other two members are UNPINNED — LIMEADE and MOCHACHINO are chronological candidates only.
- **The seven soaps (maker store, products.json live 2026-11-07):** Unscented (2017-06-09, $14.99, unavailable); Limeade (2017-08-04, $16.99, tart lime/orange/lemon, essential oils); Mochachino (2018-11-01, $15.99, coffee/chocolate/sugar/vanilla/caramel — intentional misspelling, maker-attested); Classic Country Barbershop (2021-11-28, $15.99, bergamot/basil/oakmoss/white patchouli/powder — formerly "Classic Country Barber", rename date unpinned); Backyard (2022-06-22, $15.99, grass/woods/bergamot/oakmoss/pepper/raspberry/vanilla); Pretty Polly LE Halloween seasonal (2025-09-21 first run, returned Sept 2026, $16.99, grave soil/moss/cedarwood/plum/blood orange/jasmine/amber); Sangria Nocturne (2026-05-20, $16.99, dark fruits/citrus/red wine).
- **One shared vegan base** across all seven: Stearic Acid, Water, Palm Oil, Shea Butter, Coconut Oil, Potassium Hydroxide, Sodium Hydroxide + Fragrance Oil (Limeade: Essential Oils; Unscented: none). Scent is the identity; base is never a split.
- **Aftershave/toner line (2026 expansion):** Limeade Aftershave Toner (alcohol-free, 2026-06-24, $15.99; lime + creamy coconut + aloe); Sangria Nocturne Aftershave Toner (alcohol-free, 2026-05-20, $15.99); Pretty Polly Aftershave Splash (alcohol, 2026-09-10). No aftershaves for Backyard, Classic Country Barbershop, Mochachino, or Unscented. **Pre-shave oils:** TSM Lime + TSM Natural (2026-10-05, $10.99) — the newest house products.
- **Maker:** Music City Suds, Nashville (contract maker, P-04 — brand of record stays The Shave Mercantile). Full record: lineages.md Run 2026-11-07 section. Dataset footprint is thin and single-owner (Backyard 7 rows / PhilosphicalZombie; Classic Country Barber 3 rows / PhilosphicalZombie; Limeade 3 rows / Allisnotwellin; Mochachino/Pretty Polly/Sangria Nocturne zero rows) — validators promote on maker evidence + attestation, not volume.

## Catie's Bubbles scent-catalog audit (learner run 2026-11-07) [catalog]

**Scope:** full-corpus census over sotd_2016–2026_full.json (240,951 rows): 6,993 CB rows / 1,781 distinct strings, normalized to 119 clean scent clusters ≥5 rows; plus TryThatSoap collection mining (62 CB collections, TTS 112–2636) and two Wayback CDX slices of catiesbubbles.com. Catalog state 2026-11-07: 493 CB entries (219 verified, 119 verified soap). Full census + TTS list + CDX inventory saved in the goal's hidden_files (`cb_scent_audit_2026-11-07.txt`).

**Brand facts (ShaveSplash interview with Chris Cullen, 2026-03-19, primary-ish):** founded **2013** by **Chris Cullen**, named after his daughter Catie; one-person handmade operation ("every soap/aftershave/perfume is made by hand by one person, me"); NJ / Jersey influence — Blugere and Pine Barrens are the most Jersey-shaped scents; Barnegat Bay = fairly traditional bay rum. **Le Piment de la Vie launched 2014**, still bestseller in 2023. **322 "started as a joke**, yet it became a real fragrance." **Top of the World made with Matt at Teton Shaves.** Newer scent worlds named: Top of the World, Daydream. **No new scents imminent as of March 2026** ("creative deserts… nothing close to completion"). Bases over the years: Italian, French, French+ (jojoba oil, pricier), Luxury (cream), Premium (kokum + mango seed butter), Luxury Hard Soap 2oz — base is an era/format attribute, never the scent identity; the 2oz Luxury Hard Soap line is maker-attested via CDX popup images (LTV, LPV, UJG, Spanish Holiday, RDM, BB, AMD, Irish Coffee, PGF, TP, 95LL, Waterlyptus).

**The "CB <X>" abbreviation pattern (for the matcher):** posters abbreviate the brand AND the scent freely — LPV = Le Piment de la Vie (15 rows/10a), LTV = La Terre Verte, LMR = Le Marché du Rasage (14 rows/8a), CT Shade = Connecticut Shade (45 rows/3a), QCV = Quatre Cent Vingt (see below), CB V / CB X / CB 322 / CB Blugere / CB Porch Drinks / CB Two to Mango. **All abbreviations are scent-scoped, never auto-expanded outside CB strings** — "CB" itself is field-dependent (razor field "CB razor" = Karve Christopher Bradley, glossary.md). French names are routinely misspelled (Le Marche Du Rassage, Le Piement, Menage a Lavende, "Quatre Cent Vignt") — fold on the canonical spelling, never create entries on misspellings.

**QCV = Quatre Cent Vingt — RESOLVED [catalog]:** QCV is the initialism (Quatre-Cent-Vingt = 420). B&B 411774: "Quatre Cent Vignt translates to '4:20' and smells like… flowering cannabis"; Fragrantica: Quatre Cent Vingt launched 2017, cannabis top note, "a soft floral, but unisex fougere accented with the smell of a young cannabis flower" (maker's own note). Dataset QCV: 9 rows / 5 authors, 2016–2018 (vacaloca: "Very cannabis up front but then evolves into a fougere"; eaterofworld's 4/20 posts). **Fold all QCV clusters into the Quatre Cent Vingt entries** (soap/aftershave/fragrance); never a separate QCV entry. Note the launch-date tension: QCV soap strings predate Fragrantica's 2017 EdP launch — the soap may predate the fragrance launch; do not cite 2017 as the soap's launch.

**Promotion-ready gaps (no verified entry in any format; evidence on file):**
- **Sunset** — 69 rows / 6a, 2017–2026, TTS 560; catalog has only a corroborated soap cluster (`unverified/soap/catie-s-bubbles-sunset`) + unverified aftershave/fragrance clusters. Promote.
- **Quatre Cent Vingt** — 26 rows / 12a, 2016–2026 (plus 9 QCV rows folding in); attested soap + aftershave clusters. Promote; add QCV as alias.
- **Siberian Sunrise** — 9 rows / 5a (pppork, assistantpigkeeper, Tonality, ChocoTacoKid, sahenders), 2020–2021, soap + aftershave splash, Luxury Cream base, TTS 2460. Promote.
- **Muguet** — 4 rows / 3a + maker himself (u/C_Bubbles, Lather Games 2020, "CB luxury cream… This scent is…"), TTS 825, lily of the valley (J33pGuy13 note). Promote on maker attestation + TTS.
- **Villain's Rose / The Villain's Rose** — 8 rows, all wallygator88, aftershave only, 2022–2024, TTS 463; "Going to miss this scent" (2022-09-30) reads retired. Single-author but TTS-backed; promote the aftershave, no soap entry owed.
- **Eau de Thé** — 12 rows / 5a, 2019–2026, TTS 1568 + maker image files (Eau de The ASL/EdP/Top 2021). Promote; attested aftershave cluster exists.
- **Summer Solstice** — 10 rows / 4a, 2019–2024, unverified clusters only. Promote on attestation.
- **Caged** — 6 rows / 6a, 2017–2020; attested cluster (`unverified/soap/catie-s-bubbles-caged`) only. Promote.
- **Pommes Au Four** — 1 row (nickasimpson, 2020-06-15, PIF'd sample) + TTS 406; unverified soap cluster exists. Lead-grade for promotion (one row) — promote only if the validator accepts TTS + one row, else leave.
- **Greek Fire** — 8 rows, all PhilosphicalZombie, 2022–2026, Luxury Cream soap, myrtle + menthol ("icy hot"); corroborated cluster exists AND maker image file `GreekFireSoapTop.jpg` (CDX batch 1). Promote.
- **Dark Roast** — 13 rows, all FireDragonMonkey, aftershave only, 2024–2026; notes: coffee/cookies/sandalwood (2026-07-24). Single-author; unverified aftershave cluster exists. Promote the aftershave on sustained single-owner use, or leave attested — validator call.

**WCS (West Coast Shaving) exclusives — CB-made [catalog]:** three Luxury Cream scents, TTS 1086/1087/1089: **Chypre** (many rows, jpq3366/shredsofmetal/chefkoolaid/bloodcrab/AntwonPeachFuzz, 2019–2023, soap + splash; jpq3366's note "Excited to finally try Catie's Bubbles soap" on a "WCS Chypre jojoba shaving cream" row pins the maker), **Oriental** (many rows, reguyw_nothingtolose/Mochee38/cowzilla3, 2019–2021; a matching EdT existed per reguyw_nothingtolose), **Cologne** (1 row, 2019). Catalog has verified `west-coast-shaving-chypre` entries (soap/aftershave/fragrance) — validators add CB as the manufacture attribute on the cream-era entries. **The 2023 "WCS Chypre (Duck Fat)" row is a different base era** — do not fold the duck-fat variant into the CB-made Luxury Cream entry without a base-era split. No dedicated WCS Oriental/Cologne CB entries exist — owed on TTS + dataset evidence.

**Leads (do NOT promote on current evidence):**
- **"Guy Noir"** — 11 rows, ALL Glass_Procedure7497, soap only, 2025-05 → 2026-04; zero web hits, no TTS collection, no catalog cluster. His other CB strings show the pattern: he owns exactly three CB soaps (Tonsorium 184×, Connecticut Shade 60× + CT Shade 41×, Guy Noir 11×) and appends family descriptors ("Tonsorium Fougere" 17× = Tonsorium, a fougère — fold to Tonsorium, never a separate scent). Guy Noir may be a real 2025 CB scent or his pet name for a third tub; Chiseled Face's verified Guy Noir is a DIFFERENT brand — never fold CB strings into it. Re-open only on a tub-label photo or a second author.

- **Image-only aftershave-tonic scents** (2017-era files, zero dataset strings): Margaux, Frenchy, Pine, Spruce, Tobacco, Tonga Caramel, Lavenille, Contempler — plus bath/body names (Caramel Latte, Creme of the Crop, Golden Honey, Honey Ale, Asian Pear, Frozen Grass, Green Orange, Hot Buttered Rum, Karmic Spa, Kentish Rain). These are maker-side-only names; do not create shaving entries on image filenames alone.
- **"Irish Cream" as a CB name = poster paraphrase only** (B&B 2015 "Caties Bubbles Soap Irish Cream", coffee + whiskey + cream) — zero CB "Irish Cream" dataset strings; the product is Irish Coffee. Never create an entry.

DFTR is NOT a meetup product (see lineages.md DFTR entry).
