## Run 2026-11-11 ~01:06–01:50 CST — B&M Sunny Lane + Figarose arcs RESOLVED (backlog (c4-narrow-9), (c4-narrow-10); rotation: product lines — prior runs were the B&M retail-afterlife cluster)

**Sunny Lane [catalog]:**
- **Launch ~Mar 2020, Excelsior base.** First in hands 2020-03-28 (assistantpigkeeper: "the most recent children's themed offering from B&M"; Terciel1976's Maggard sample box arrived the same morning; broala's samples arrived Mar 28). MalthusTheShaver tags "Excelsior base" 2020-03-31. Scent notes (maker copy via Naked Armor): bergamot, vetiver, sweet clover, oakmoss — "peppery, green, and sparklingly fresh"; spring positioning; Sesame Workshop donation per jar/bottle sold.
- **Dataset: 283 rows / 59 authors** — 2020 (140) → 2021 (76) → 2022–2025 trickle (5–12/yr) → 2026 (37). The 2026 spike is author-driven (RedMosquitoMM ×8, hugbckt ×4, FredBikes9876 ×4, bmac92 ×3, SamIAmShepard alphabet run) — NO 2026 purchase/re-release narration in any Jan–Feb 2026 note. Do not cite a 2026 re-release.
- **Omnibus era:** Naked Armor listing "Sunny Lane Shaving Soap (Omnibus Base)" created 2025-10-08, published 2026-01-05 (same Jan 5 push as Figarose), sold out, $27. Maker copy on the listing: the fragrance was **REWORKED** for the Omnibus version — "softer and a little fruitier, less strikingly green, and less astringent" — so Omnibus Sunny Lane is a reformulated scent, not a base-only migration (same-scent-different-base key still applies, with the scent change noted). Dataset attestation of the Omnibus version in hands is exactly ONE row (RedMosquitoMM 2026-06-26, "Sunny Lane - Omnibus Shaving Soap"). Maker store: no Sunny Lane product at all (85 products) — retailer-only now (NA).
- **No Sunny Lane EdT:** the single fragrance-field row (wallygator88 2020-06-01) is a notes-mention false positive (fragrance field = Motif Olfactif Vetu de Verte). No fragrance entry owed.
- **Catalog:** verified soap + aftershave exist (`soap/barrister-and-mann-sunny-lane` — evidence: store-type NA Omnibus page + nmrs corroboration "barrister and mann sunny day" → Sunny Lane; `aftershave/barrister-and-mann-sunny-lane` via set cross-validation). Parallel verified `aftershave/barrister-and-mann-b-m-sunny-lane` duplicate (same dedup pattern as Figarose / Fougere Classique).

**Figarose [catalog]:**
- **Launch ~Aug 2021, Soft Heart base.** First in hands 2021-08-16 (mammothben); Aresmsu 2021-08-19 "Figarose showed up yesterday". Naked Armor copy confirms: "Originally released in 2021 as part of Barrister and Mann's experimental Soft Heart line, which would go on to form the foundation for the current Omnibus shaving soap base." Scent notes: unripe green fig, rose, lemon, fir, driftwood.
- **Dataset: 203 rows / 35 authors** — 2021 (48) → steady 19–46/yr through 2026 (46). Product-scoped base tags: Soft Heart 12 (9 "Soft Heart" + 3 "SoftHeart", incl. MalthusTheShaver 2021-09-07); ZERO scoped Omnibus tags — the 20 row-level "omnibus" hits are contamination (the one field-scoped hit is a Fougère Gothique row in SamIAmShepard's alphabet run). The 2026 spike is again author-driven (SamIAmShepard B&M alphabet run Jun 21; Environmental-Gap380 repeat use; partyman97_3 Jan 27 "work my way through my den"; expoqeteer Mar 4 "soaps I bought last year"). No 2026 re-release narration.
- **Omnibus era at retail:** NA soap listing created 2026-01-05, published same day, sold out, $27, INCI = Omnibus verbatim (cupuaçu/goat milk/marshmallow/slippery elm); splash listing created 2026-01-05, **available:true, $27 — the splash is CURRENT retail stock, not sold out** (corrects the 23:06 run's "both sold out" gloss). The Jan 5, 2026 NA publication of both Figarose listings + Sunny Lane = a coordinated NA B&M Omnibus listing push, not a maker launch (maker store has no Figarose either). Whether the Omnibus soap is a reissue or a listing migration of 2021–22 stock is UNPINNED from the dataset (zero Omnibus-tagged Figarose rows in hands); the INCI proves the LISTED product is Omnibus-base.
- **Catalog:** verified soap (photo evidence — tub lid reads "Barrister and Mann — figarose", reddit 1pst40t) + aftershave (set cross-validation) exist; parallel verified `aftershave/barrister-and-mann-b-m-figarose` duplicate. Unverified Soft Heart clusters fold in.

**Method notes:** (1) Row-level base-tag counts are contaminated — "Reserve" 11/22 and "Omnibus" 20 for these scents were all other-products-in-the-row; only field-scoped counts (base word inside the same soap/aftershave string as the scent) are evidence. (2) A retailer's created_at + published_at pair dates the LISTING, and a same-day publication cluster across scents (Jan 5, 2026) is a retailer push, not a launch wave — the maker store's absence + zero in-hands tags keep the reissue-vs-migration question open. (3) A scent-profile rework note on maker copy ("rework the original fragrance profile") is a scent change, not a base change — record it as such even when the scent+base key stays.

## Run 2026-11-11 ~03:06–03:50 CST — B&M Special Sessions Series RESOLVED (backlog (c4-narrow-13); rotation: product lines — prior runs were the B&M retail-afterlife cluster, Sunny Lane, Figarose)

**Special Sessions Series (2026) [catalog]:** a four-scent blues-tribute line, all four authored by outside perfumer **Cécile Hua** (see people.md) — a first for B&M, whose scents are normally Will Carius's own compositions. Maker copy (barristerandmann.com, live 2026-11-11) + dataset census (full corpus, field-scoped):
- **Launch arc: Maggard meetup exclusive → maker-site general release.** Presented exclusively at the Maggard Razors meetup, April 2026, sets sold to attendees while supplies lasted (YouTube unboxing, sbarbarsi, ~Oct 24, 2026: "presented exclusively at the Maggard Razors meetup last April 2026... A few months later, the four sets are back in stock exclusively on the manufacturer's website"). Maker store listings: soaps + splashes created 2026-09-21/22, published 2026-09-22; soap samples created 2026-10-16, published 2026-10-21. **Dataset signature is launch-lag, not meetup-lag:** ZERO April–September 2026 strings for any scent — first in-hands rows are all October (Waits 2026-10-09, Two Walters 2026-10-10, King 2026-10-15, Kanno 2026-10-21), i.e. the meetup exclusive left no SOTD trace until the September general release. Do not date any Sessions string to April on the meetup alone.
- **The four scents (maker copy, verbatim notes):**
  - **Kanno** — homage to Yoko Kanno (Cowboy Bebop; "almost single-handedly dragged Western jazz and blues into the world of Japanese anime"); shiso leaf, grapefruit, pink pepper, geranium; "a spectacular twist on the classic Eau de Cologne structure"; rated highest of the four in blind smelling at the April meetup. Dataset: 4 rows / 4 authors, all Oct 21–Nov 6, 2026 (DealeyLama, RenegadeLamp, Tetriside, mpark6288). Soap + splash both attested.
  - **King** — tribute to B.B. King, "the man and his guitars" (all guitars named "Lucille"); leather, vetiver, sandalwood, oak, amber. Dataset: 6 genuine rows / 5 authors, Oct 15–Nov 9, 2026. Soap + aftershave both attested.
  - **Two Walters** — Big Walter (Horton) + Little Walter (Jacobs), harmonica players, "no relation"; rosemary, cedar, sea salt, caramel, wood smoke; Will's personal favorite ("If you can only choose one, this is the one to try!"). Dataset: 2 rows / 2 authors (gcgallant 2026-10-10, Tetriside 2026-11-08). Soap + aftershave both attested.
  - **Waits** — Tom Waits, "the Bard of the Bizarre"; roasted coffee, balsam fir, pine sap, black salt. Dataset: 2 rows / 2 authors, both 2026-10-09 (gcgallant, _walden_). Soap + aftershave both attested. The Barbershop Duet reviewed the Waits soap (S1 E871, ~Nov 7, 2026).
- **"Five great musicians" = four sets:** the unboxing's "five" counts Two Walters as two men (Big + Little Walter). Never cite a fifth Sessions scent.
- **Pricing/availability (maker store, live 2026-11-11):** soap $27.49, splash $24.99, sample $3.99; 12 listings total. Kanno soap SOLD OUT, King soap SOLD OUT; Waits + Two Walters soaps available; all four splashes available; Kanno sample sold out, other three samples available. Naked Armor carries NO Sessions product (1000-product scan) — maker-site exclusive so far.
- **BASE UNPINNED [catalog]:** maker copy names no base and shows no INCI; no retailer carries the line (so no retailer INCI either). $27.49 sits above the core Omnibus soap price ($24.99 at the maker store) — LE pricing, not base evidence; do NOT read Omnibus from the price. The Duet Waits review has a spoken "Base and Ingredients" chapter (2:33) — a transcript read (live-browser job) or a tub-label INCI photo is the route. Until then, validators: create the entries WITHOUT a base-era tag; never default them to Omnibus.
  - **RESOLUTION 2026-11-14 ~17:20 CST (handoff addendum) [catalog]: (c4-narrow-16) RESOLVED — Sessions soaps ARE the Omnibus base, by INCI identity.** The Barbershop Duet Waits review (S1 E871, youtube.com/watch?v=lpOUDoMqLhQ; live-browser read 17:13–17:20 CST) shows an on-screen graphic in the 2:33 "Base and Ingredients" chapter: "The BASE: A tallow formula" + the full INCI. The spoken audio names no base (it talks about Tom Waits and "Cecil Hua"); the word "Omnibus" appears NOWHERE in the video or its transcript. But the INCI is the Omnibus list, ingredient-for-ingredient in the same order: Aqua, Potassium Stearate, Glycerin, Potassium Tallowate, Fragrance, Sodium Stearate, Garcinia indica seed butter (Potassium Kokum Butterate), Polyacrylamidomethylpropane Sulfonic Acid, Cocos Nucifera Fruit Juice (Coconut Milk), Sodium Tallowate, Potassium Ricinoleate, Sodium Lactate, Butyrospermum Parkii (Shea) Butter, Tetrasodium Ethylenediamine Disuccinate, Xanthan Gum, Theobroma Grandiflorum (Cupuacu) Seed Butter, Sodium Cocoa (Kokum) Butterate, Potassium Cocoate, Hydroxyethylcellulose, Sodium Ricinoleate, Saccharide Isomerate, Persea Gratissima (Avocado) Oil, Sodium Cocoate, Althaea Officinalis (Marshmallow) Root Extract, Ulmus rubra (Slippery Elm) Extract. It matches the Naked Armor Season of the Witch Omnibus listing essentially verbatim, and carries every distinctive Omnibus marker (Rheocare polymer, cupuaçu, saccharide isomerate, hydroxyethylcellulose, marshmallow + slippery elm, avocado oil, coconut milk) — none of which exist in Glissant/Excelsior — and no lanolin and no oat surfactant (so NOT Excelsior). The two apparent deltas are within documented Omnibus listing variance: no Goat Milk Powder (also absent from the NA Vespers and Season of the Witch Omnibus listings) and Tetrasodium vs Trisodium Ethylenediamine Disuccinate (both forms appear across NA Omnibus listings), as do "Sodium Ricinoleate" vs "Sodium Ricinolate". **Catalog guidance:** validators MAY tag Sessions soap entries as Omnibus base. **Naming caveat:** no source calls the Sessions line "Omnibus" by name — the Duet graphic says only "A tallow formula" — so cite the formula match, never a maker Omnibus naming for the line. **Source grade:** reviewer on-screen graphic (secondary transcription of the tub label), not a maker page or a legible tub photo — the physical tub's small print was illegible in the 3:52 unboxing shot. The tub title itself is confirmed: "BARRISTER AND MANN / SHAVING SOAP / SESSIONS SERIES: / WAITS", 4 oz. Do not cite the hosts' spoken spellings ("Cecil Hua", "Tom Weights").
- **Catalog gaps [catalog]:** verified soaps exist for King, Two Walters, Waits (bulk harvest 2026-10-20, evidence = maker store pages) — **Kanno soap has NO entry at all** (not even unverified) despite 4 rows / 4 authors + a live maker page: promotion-ready. **All four Sessions aftershaves are OWED** (no entries of any status): Kanno splash (DealeyLama 2026-10-21, mpark6288 2026-11-06), King aftershave (arcaderehab ×2), Two Walters aftershave (gcgallant 2026-10-10), Waits aftershave (gcgallant 2026-10-09) — all with maker-page evidence. No Sessions EdT exists anywhere checked; no fragrance entry owed. Naming: canonical series title is **"Sessions Series"** (maker title); poster variants "Session Series" (arcaderehab) and "Shaving Sessions Series" (expoqeteer) are aliases — see product_aliases.md. Dedup watch: the verified King soap entry's id carries the long form `sessions-series-shaving-soap-king`; the unverified `soap/barrister-and-mann-king-croap` cluster folds into it.

**Method notes:** (1) A meetup-exclusive launch can leave zero dataset trace for months — the Sessions census proves the general-release date, not the meetup date, is what dataset rows attest; a "first use" is a first-USE date, never a launch date, for channel-exclusive releases. (2) A $2.50 price premium over the house base price is LE pricing until an INCI says otherwise — price is not base evidence. (3) "Five musicians, four sets" is a counting trap resolved by reading the tribute list, not by hunting a missing fifth product.

**Findings (established unless noted):**
- **(c4-narrow-79) RESOLVED [catalog]:** the "SKU evolution" is a retailer-vs-maker scheme difference, NOT a maker SKU change. 2015 Maggard: soap **BM-CHYPRE**, splash **BM-AS-CHYPRE** (Maggard-assigned, BM- prefix; snapshot-read 15:06). Maker's own store, live: soap **SS-LGC** (handle `le-grand-chypre-special-edition-shaving-soap`, created 2015-11-16), splash **AS-LGC** (handle `le-grand-chypre-limited-edition-alcohol-aftershave-splash`, created 2015-11-16) — both sold out. Current Maggard: soap **BM-SOAP-CHYPRE** (live page, Omnibus base, $24.99, sold out). So Maggard's own scheme evolved BM-CHYPRE → BM-SOAP-CHYPRE (presumably with the 4oz/Omnibus relisting); B&M's own SKUs are unchanged since 2015. Validators: cite SS-LGC/AS-LGC for maker-side, BM-CHYPRE/BM-AS-CHYPRE for the 2015 Maggard listing, BM-SOAP-CHYPRE for the current Maggard listing — never read a maker change out of the BM- prefix.
- **(c4-narrow-78) NARROWED [catalog]:** maker-side LGC launch bracket is now **Nov 16–20, 2015**. Maker evidence: the B&M store's own LGC soap + splash listings were both created **2015-11-16** (products.json, live); the maker-site LGC splash page was first captured **2015-11-23 08:06 UTC** (CDX, filter form — see method note); the Maggard drop was **Nov 20, 2015, noon EST** (resolved 15:06). Daily Lather's history page corroborates the month ("Le Grand Chypre | Nov 2015") and the Glissant debut ("starting with Le Grand Chypre a new formulation was debuted, named Glissant"). Fragrantica ("launched in 2014") and silloria ("2014 Release of Le Grand Chypre") are the page-addition-date trap — do NOT cite a 2014 LGC launch. Residual: the maker's own Nov 2015 announcement (blog/newsletter/B&B vendor post) for the exact day; the bracket is five days wide.
- **(c4-narrow-80) STILL OPEN, maker-CDX negative added [catalog]:** maker-site CDX (products/* + `original:.*fireside.*` filter, control-verified) returns ONLY the original Fireside page — `products/fireside-tallow-shaving-soap-essential-oil-fragrance`, first capture **2015-02-06** (200; .oembed 2015-01-06) — no separate Glissant-era Fireside page was ever captured on the maker domain in 2015–16. The re-release likely sold through the original page or via Maggard first. Bracket stays **(Nov 2015, Dec 24, 2015]**. Dataset adds nothing: first 2016 Fireside soap use is 2016-05-15 (MMCZ86), inside the "available until March 20, 2016" window but no pin. Daily Lather lists Fireside as "Dec 2014" (the original release only) — never cite it for the re-release.
- **(c4-narrow-76) RESOLVED — switch premise RETIRED [catalog]:** the maggards.com 2021–2023 distinct-URL census (CDX, collapse=urlkey, control-verified) is alarm-company ONLY: WordPress pages `/company/`, `/contact/`, `/medical-alert/`, `/monitoring/`, `/residential/`, `/surveillance/`, `/testimonials/`, Easy Testimonials + Meta Slider plugins, "anya-installable" theme — ZERO shaving-store URLs. Combined with the 14:45 handoff (zero shaving captures through 2020; `/medical-alert` captured 2022-05-18), the premise is answered for 2015–2023: **maggards.com never became the shaving store in that window**; the store is maggardrazors.com. "When did maggards.com switch" is retired as a false premise — never cite a maggards.com absence for any pre-switch date, and never cite a switch date. Residual (live-browser only): the live current state of maggards.com today (sandbox DNS sinkhole blocks a direct check).
- **(c4-narrow-14) NARROWED [catalog]:** the Sunny Lane rework was in retail by **Oct 8, 2025** (Naked Armor listing created; NA copy: B&M "decided to rework the original Sunny Lane fragrance profile to make it softer and a little fruitier, less strikingly green, and less astringent"). The maker store carries NO Sunny Lane at all (85 products, live) — Naked Armor is the ONLY retail trace, and it lists the soap only (no splash). Dataset: 256 Sunny Lane rows; hugbckt already tags "(original release)" on **2025-10-20** — the community was distinguishing versions by Oct 2025; souleater7173 "First use of the new Sunny Lane" **2025-11-01**; first explicit Omnibus tag **2026-06-26** (RedMosquitoMM, "Sunny Lane - Omnibus Shaving Soap"). The maker ANNOUNCEMENT date stays unpinned — the bracket is now (Mar 2020 original, Oct 8, 2025 retail] with in-hands by Nov 1, 2025. Caution: souleater7173 calls the new version "Very green" — do not over-read a single first-impression note against the rework copy.

The scent returned in four formats 2022–2023; entries owed where missing.
- **CORRECTION — Snippid brush watch store [catalog]:** the six Snippid brushes (6239mc–6244mc, v31 Fanchurian, $225) live on **houseofmammoth.com**, NOT chiselandhound.com — the C&H 725-product scan is clean of Snippid because the brushes are HoM listings. Status otherwise unchanged: only **6244mc available:true**; Snippid soap/splash/balm/EdP all available. Prior runs checked the wrong store's products.json for the brush; the watch now reads the HoM store.
- **Watches rechecked (all NO CHANGE unless noted):** CL Parfums wind-down (e) — 12 products, same four TBD titles, all variants available:true, $70/2 oz; bulk updated_at 2026-11-12T17:07:22-06:00. Black Ship store-death (b) — 67 products / 56 available, latest created_at still 2026-06-14; Final Voyage Collector's Set available:true; bulk updated_at 2026-11-12T18:07:24-05:00. Battle Brothers — legacy "Non US" 100-pack STILL LISTED ($16.20, available:true); zero BattleSisters mentions in the 11-product store catalog. HoM Tusk Indigo sample (a1) — 19 variants; Indigo available:true, $6.00; Mull/Kryptonite v2/Everett available; Fú Dào sold out; Tobacconist available. Figarose (c4-narrow-15) — splash available:true $27 at NA; soap sold out. Mull restock (c4-narrow-24) — soap available:true $29; EdP + splash sold out. v32 (c4-narrow-27) — still 2 rows / 1 author (arcaderehab 2026-11-08 + 2026-11-11). B&M Hand & Body Soap (c4-narrow-11) — 12 listings, same titles, no new listing. Hua × B&M program (c4-narrow-17) — Hua on 12 listings (8 Sessions + 4 soap samples), still a one-off; B&M store 85 products, newest created 2026-10-16.
- **Notes-mining slice ≥ 2026-11-12:** 0 new rows (dataset 241,562 rows, max thread_date 2026-11-11). Next slice ≥ 2026-11-12.

**Method notes:** (1) A mid-URL CDX wildcard (`host/*fireside*`) silently returns empty — it does NOT substring-match; the valid form is `url=host/products/*` + `filter=original:.*fireside.*`. The earlier empty B&M fireside/chypre CDX results were methodologically invalid, not findings — the filter-form query is what establishes the maker-site negative. (2) A retailer's SKU prefix is its own scheme — BM-CHYPRE vs SS-LGC is Maggard-vs-maker, not a product change; compare schemes, not strings. (3) A Shopify store's products.json gives maker-side created_at for a launch bracket even when the maker site never had a captured product page — the Nov 16 created_at + Nov 23 first capture + Nov 20 retail drop triangulate the launch to five days. (4) A collab brush's store follows the collab's publisher, not the brush maker — C&H-made HoM collab brushes are HoM listings; check the publisher's store first.

**Method notes:** (1) A "first use" per scent across a balm line reveals the rollout order — the per-scent first-use dates (Seville Dec 2019 → Cool/Terror Jul 2020 → Bay Rum Sep 2020 → LGC/Lavanille/Cheshire/Eigengrau/Roam Dec 2020–Jan 2021 → Waves Aug 2022 → FG Sep 2025) are the launch wave map; a single listing's created_at never dates the line. (3) A CDX 404 capture is a death certificate with a date — the two Black Tide ghost handles died before Oct 21, 2026; a 200 capture the same day proves the tonic was alive then. (4) Shopify's hourly bulk-touch updated_at is not an event — but a product-count change inside one bulk-touch interval IS (67→66 between two reads ~90 minutes apart).

**Method notes:** (1) A CDX empty must be validated against a known-positive control in the same run, or it means nothing; a "Temporarily Offline" page immediately after a `[]` invalidates the `[]`. (2) Dataset first-use can LAG firsthand forum posts by weeks (Roam balm: B&B Dec 18, 2020 vs dataset Jan 6, 2021) — a dataset-first-use date is an upper bound on first in-hands, not the launch; cross-check B&B for a wave's scents. (3) A Shopify created_at CAN pin a launch day when it survives un-re-listed (Seville/Unscented balms, Jul 15, 2020) — the re-listing rule cuts both ways: created_at is a re-listing only when dataset use predates it; when first use follows created_at, the listing date stands. (4) A title-match count and a handle-match count are different instruments — "Aleppo Soap" carries no "Hand & Body Soap" in its title; count by handle when the category is a handle prefix. (5) A store homepage that never lists a category can't date that category's products — check what the homepage actually links before reading absence as evidence.

The PLAIN `/products/le-grand-chypre-special-edition-shaving-soap` URL was NEVER captured as a 200: uncollapsed exact-URL query (www and non-www return identically — CDX normalizes the host) shows exactly three Nov–Dec 2015 captures, all 404s (Nov 26 02:33; Dec 3 03:37; Dec 3 06:27 with trailing `?`). The domain-wide catch-all confirms the complete soap-slug inventory for Nov–Dec 2015: the two Nov 23 collection 200s, the Nov 26 + Dec 3 plain-URL 404s, one Dec 3 cyber-monday-2015 404, Dec 24 collection-variant 404s, and the Nov 26 .oembed 404 — nothing else. Session validated: the Nov 23 08:06:10 splash-page 200 (`le-grand-chypre-limited-edition-alcohol-aftershave-splash?`) returned in the same queries, so the session was not flapping. **The 07:06 run's collapsed query was correct for the URL it asked — it asked the wrong URL form.** The backlog's "first captures Nov 23, 2015 08:05 (collection variants, 200)" was correct all along; the "sold out within ~3 days" reading stands (plain URL 404 by Nov 26). **Method rule:** a collapsed exact-URL CDX query answers only for that exact URL — Shopify collection-variant URLs (`/collections/<name>/products/<slug>`) are SEPARATE urlkeys with their own capture histories; always run the collections filter + domain-wide catch-all before concluding a Shopify product page was never captured live.

**Method notes:** (1) A wrong-slug control query returning `[]` voids nothing about the session — it is an operator error; re-run the control with the exact proven URL before drawing any session conclusion. (2) An IA "Temporarily Offline" HTML page is not a CDX result — retry the query, never parse the HTML as an empty. (3) A single-product .js fetch returning 0 bytes is transient until retried — the Figarose retry returned HTTP 200 with the variant inventory_quantity still 1. (4) A retailer's "no LGC page survives" is meaningful only when the same domain demonstrably stocked the brand in the same era (ShaveRevolution's Apr 2015 B&M captures) — otherwise it is just an uncaptured domain. (5) A same-day availability flip brackets the sell-out to the interval between the two watch reads — record the bracket, not a single timestamp.

The page was a zombie listing for 7+ years: title and price edited, stock never returned. **CORRECTION to the 13:06 framing:** "back-ordered, ship in 10–15 days" is IB's GENERIC sold-out template text, not a special restock signal — it appears verbatim on every sold-out IB B&M page read this run (Cheshire May 2016, Fireside Dec 2015, both LGC reads). **Method notes:** (1) A collapsed CDX query shows only the FIRST capture per urlkey — for "did it restock / how long did the listing live" questions always run the exact-URL query UNCOLLAPSED; the 11:06 collapsed file made the IB LGC soap page look dead after Jan 2016 when it lived until Sep 2023. (2) A retailer's "back-ordered" wording is template text until proven otherwise — IB prints it on every sold-out B&M page (Cheshire, Fireside, LGC); never cite it as a restock expectation. (3) A mid-string CDX wildcard (`*le-grand-chypre*`) returns `[]` even when the URL exists — wildcards work only as a trailing `*` on a prefix, or not at all; re-run with the exact proven URL (same operator-error class as the 11:06 wrong-slug control). (4) A title/price change on a back-ordered page (LGC "Limited Edition" dropped, $25.95 → $26.99 by Jul 2019) is a listing edit, never a restock signal — only an Add to Cart / available variant is.

**Sources used:** full-corpus dataset census (sotd_*_full.json only, 241,198 rows, max thread_date 2026-11-12 — Cheshire+Glissant rows, 37, sorted); Daily Lather B&M history page (live read 2026-11-13 — White Label + Glissant INCIs verbatim; Cheshire row: released May 2013, Current, no per-base dates); Wayback CDX (maker Cheshire old slug `cheshire-shaving-soap-essential-oil`, exact-URL uncollapsed, 12 captures Feb 2015–Jan 2017; IB Seville slug, collapsed, 1 capture Aug 1, 2015 — both saved 2026-11-13, suffix "i"); live products.json reads (chatillonlux 12, blackshipgroomingco 66, houseofmammoth 76, battlebrothers 11, barristerandmann 85, nakedarmor pp.1–7 = 1,302, maggardrazors pp.1–14 = 3,170 — all saved 2026-11-13, suffix "i"); Naked Armor single-product .js (Figarose splash, www + no-www hosts — both 0-byte this run); catalog_merged.json Cheshire entry check.

**Findings (established unless noted):**
- **(c4-narrow-101) RESOLVED — CHESHIRE STAYED ON WHITE LABEL [catalog]:** the IB May 28, 2016 Cheshire INCI is a TRUE formula record of the product as sold then, NOT frozen copy. The dataset narrates it unanimously across NINE authors: Quadricwan 2016-06-11 ("nursing samples of this and Cheshire because I can't bring myself to buy a full tub knowing that Glissant is coming. White label performance is still ridiculously good"); hughmonstah 2016-07-19 ("get through this tub before the glissant based one gets released"); OrcasGoneWild 2016-08-16 ("B&M Cheshire (when it comes out in Glissant)"); RuggerRigger 2016-09-03 ("Great lather from the traditional white label base" — LGC "the only glissant base I have"); RealDavyJones 2016-12-23 ("hoping it will be released in the Glissant base soon"); darkfox45 2017-03-30 ("until the Glissant iteration comes out") + 2017-04-17 ("With the Glissant base coming out soon"); redcowboy93 2017-04-11 ("I told myself I wouldn't buy anymore B&M unless it was in the glissant base, but after trying a sample of Cheshire I had to... I quite like the white label base"); darkfox45 2017-04-30 ("Can't wait for the Glissant base to come out in this scent. Soon.") + 2017-05-09 ("must replace once that Glissant base comes out"); TheRealSheikYerbouti 2017-08-14 ("they will be doing Cheshire with the new glissant base... if so, count me in!!") + 2017-10-03 ("I can't order the new glissant base yet") + 2018-02-06 (still shaving his White Label puck). The structural read is confirmed against Daily Lather's verbatim INCIs: White Label = kokum butterate + cocoates, no sweet almond/safflower; Glissant = shea butterate + safflower + palm kernelate + sodium citrate, no kokum/cocoate — the IB Cheshire list is the former, exactly.
- **Glissant Cheshire launch bracket [catalog]:** first genuine in-hands **2018-06-01** (Vrain_19, soap field "Barrister and Mann Cheshire", verbatim: "this Glissant base is hands down the one that works best for me"); announced by **2018-04-19** (clothing_throwaway: old-base Groomatorium sample, "does anyone know if the new Glissant type will be less drying" — the Glissant type was known/announced by then). Bracket for first in-hands: **(2018-04-19, 2018-06-01]**. No Cheshire+Glissant rows exist between Feb 6 and Jun 1, 2018. Corroboration: shredsofmetal 2019-06-26 ("I still love the Glissant base" on Cheshire); OBeardWanKenobe 2021-01-29 ("just recently discovered my cheshire is Glissant, and not Excelsior"). Note the contrast with Seville: Seville was Glissant by 2016-09-27 (darkfox45, "The glissant base is also no joke" on Seville) — the core scents migrated to Glissant on DIFFERENT schedules; never infer one scent's base from another's.
- **Maker Cheshire page history [catalog]:** old slug `cheshire-shaving-soap-essential-oil` has 12 captures Feb 6, 2015 – Jan 22, 2017 (length drift 9,935 → 13,671 bytes by Aug 17, 2016, → 13,640 Jan 22, 2017) — NO capture exists in the Glissant-Cheshire window, so the maker page cannot corroborate the 2018 transition directly. The Aug 17, 2016 + Jan 22, 2017 captures are the remaining maker-side test of the White Label claim (content read dispatched — see new lead (c4-narrow-102) below). New slug `cheshire-shaving-soap` first captured Jul 17, 2019 (Excelsior era).
- **IB Seville second-page test — INCONCLUSIVE, not needed [catalog]:** IB's Seville page has exactly ONE capture, Aug 1, 2015 — pre-Glissant, so its INCI would be White Label too, but it cannot test post-Sep-2016 staleness (Seville was Glissant by Sep 27, 2016 and IB never re-captured the page). The pattern it does support: IB B&M pages are launch-era copy, captured once and frozen (LGC frozen Jan 2016–Jul 2019; Seville captured once Aug 2015) — which is consistent with, but no longer needed for, the Cheshire verdict, since the dataset settles it directly.
- **Catalog note [catalog]:** the catalog holds ONE verified Cheshire soap entry (`soap/barrister-and-mann-cheshire`, 29 aliases) spanning both bases, plus an unverified `soap/bm-cheshire` cluster. Per the scent+base key the White Label era (May 2013 – early 2018) and Glissant era (mid-2018 – Nov 2018 Excelsior) are different products; validators decide split vs era-annotation. Evidence routes for the White Label era: IB May 28, 2016 page (kokum/cocoate INCI) + the nine-author dataset narrative above; for the Glissant era: Vrain_19 2018-06-01 first in-hands + 2019–2021 Glissant-tagged uses.

**NEW NARROW LEAD (curiosity-compounds):** **(c4-narrow-102)** Maker Cheshire page content — read the Aug 17, 2016 and Jan 22, 2017 Wayback snapshots of the old slug: if both print the White Label INCI, the maker-side attestation of Cheshire-on-White-Label extends to Jan 2017 (browser task dispatched this run, result pending). If either prints Glissant, the dataset narrative's "still White Label" reading for that date is wrong and the bracket moves.

**Method notes:** (1) A retailer's INCI that matches the documented old-base formula ingredient-for-ingredient is a TRUE formula record for its capture date, not stale copy — staleness has to be proven by a contemporaneous contradiction (a poster using the new base while the page still prints the old), never assumed from the INCI's age. Here the contemporaneous evidence AGREES with the page. (2) Core-scent base migrations run on different schedules (Seville Glissant by Sep 2016, Cheshire not until mid-2018) — never infer one scent's base era from another scent's. (3) A title-match count is the wrong instrument when a listing's title doesn't carry the category words — B&M Hand & Body is 13 by handle-match, 12 by title-match (Aleppo Soap); always state the instrument. (4) urllib's default TLS/UA gets 403s from Shopify products.json endpoints that curl with a full Chrome UA passes — use curl for the watch fetches. (5) A 0-byte single-product .js fetch is transient until retried — but after three 0-byte attempts on two hosts, record the datum as NOT re-verified rather than re-asserting the last good value.

BOTH print the IDENTICAL White Label INCI, verbatim: Potassium Stearate, Sodium Stearate, Potassium Tallowate, Sodium Tallowate, Aqua, Glycerin, Citrus bergamia (Bergamot) Oil, Salvia sclarea (Clary Sage) Oil, Lavandula Angustifolia (Lavender) Oil, Pogostemon cablin (Patchouli) Oil, Potassium Ricinoleate, Sodium Ricinoleate, Coconut Milk, Potassium Kokum Butterate, Sodium Kokum Butterate, Butyrospermum Parkii (Shea) Butter, Potassium Cocoate, Sodium Cocoate, Lanolin, Allantoin, Silk Protein, Tocopherol Acetate — i.e. the Daily Lather White Label list plus the four Cheshire scent oils, exactly the Dickens pattern. NO "Glissant" anywhere on either page (text-searched); title "Cheshire Tallow Shaving Soap (Essential Oil)"; price $17.00 both dates; Add to Cart present both dates (in stock, actively selling — a live product page, not a zombie listing); Net wt. 4 oz. Banner-confirmed captures: Aug 17, 2016 02:13:33 and Jan 22, 2017 09:32:53. **Cheshire base arc now fully bracketed [catalog]: White Label May 2013 – early 2018** (maker-attested to Jan 22, 2017; dataset-attested to Feb 6, 2018, TheRealSheikYerbouti's White Label puck) **→ Glissant from mid-2018** (announced by Apr 19, 2018; first in-hands Jun 1, 2018, Vrain_19). The stale-copy reading is NOT supported here: unlike Dickens (where the B&B firsthand post contradicted the printed formula), the dataset narrative AGREES with the maker page at every date in the window (Sep 2016, Dec 2016, Mar/Apr 2017 posters all say White Label / Glissant still coming), and the page was live and selling, not frozen on a dead listing. The IB May 2016 page, the maker Aug 2016 + Jan 2017 pages, and nine dataset authors are now three concordant sources for Cheshire-on-White-Label through Jan 2017. Validator note stands: the single verified `soap/barrister-and-mann-cheshire` entry spans both bases; the White Label era evidence routes are now IB May 2016 + maker Aug 2016 + maker Jan 2017 + the nine-author narrative.

**Method notes:** (1) A retailer's "white-label" slug is a line name, not a base claim — MenEssentials' Fireside URL says "white-label" because that was the line, but the CONTENT decides the base: its INCI is the White Label-era list, so the page is the original listing, not the re-release. Never cite a slug's base word as formula evidence. (2) A `curl` http:000 (connection failure) on one CDX query while the control in the same batch returns clean is a transport failure for that query, not an IA outage and not a negative — the MenEssentials `/*`-form query failed three times at the connection level, then the matchType=domain form returned clean. Vary the query form before declaring a route blocked. (3) A homepage's "Our Picks" rotates (Adagio on sale Nov 18, gone Nov 28; Kyovu appears Nov 28) — a single homepage read is a snapshot of a rotating slot, never the full lineup; absence of a product from one homepage read is weak even before the LGC control. (4) The Tusk-sample "Fú Dào sold out / Tobacconist available" watch lines refer to VARIANTS of the 1/2oz Tusk sample product (19 variants), not standalone products — verify at the variant level before reporting a HoM change.

- **(c4-narrow-117) RESOLVED — SAME PLACE, different description; no second move [catalog].** The Hartwick assessment roll lists two adjacent Carius parcels: 129.00-1-38.01 (105 Vac farmland, 85.72 acres) and 129.00-1-39.243 (240 Rural res, 39.84 acres), both owners "Carius Harold P Jr / Carius Paula (A)", mailing address 128 Persons Rd, Hartwick, NY 13348 — total 125.56 acres. 128 Persons Rd geocodes ~5.5 miles SW of Cooperstown village center — exactly Will's "just outside of Cooperstown" (cafleurebon profile, 2018-03-30). (2) A forum thread's date GAP over the launch window is itself the finding — the absence of Nov 2015 posts in B&B 467993 exhausts that route more cleanly than a failed keyword search. (3) When two watch snapshots use different filename stems (`maggard_` vs `maggardrazors_`), a glob diff silently compares a full set against an empty one — verify the glob matched before reporting "zero flips". (4) A products.json availability MIX differs between maker store and retailer (B&M balms: 6 avail maker-side vs 3 at Maggard) — census counts are the comparable figure, mixes are channel state.

Never record a wildcard `[]` as a validated empty; exact-URL queries with a proven-positive control are the only CDX empties that count. **Method notes:** (1) A partial Shopify products.json pull is not a catalog census — TRC's catalog is 3,797 products, so the 09:06 run's 8-page (2,000-product) pull was incomplete; always page until a short/empty page before claiming "exactly one" of anything. (2) A retailer's sold-out-retention pattern is itself evidence: when a store keeps 467 sold-out listings (including a same-batch sold-out sibling), a product's absence from the complete catalog means never listed, not sold out — but only after the catalog pull is proven complete. (3) A Wayback CDX `[]` returned adjacent to a service outage is not a negative — the Archive was "Temporarily Offline" on the very next query; treat outage-adjacent empties as inconclusive and re-check when the service recovers. (4) A guessed Shopify CDN store-ID path 404s prove nothing — never cite a guessed asset URL as a negative.

- **B&M Omnibus — maker-side INCI, captured [catalog]:** B&M's Seville *sample* listing publishes the full Omnibus soap INCI (the full-size soap listing does not): Aqua, Potassium Stearate, Glycerin, Potassium Tallowate, Potassium Kokum Butterate, Sodium Stearate, Polyacrylamidomethylpropane Sulfonic Acid, Cocos nucifera Fruit Juice (Coconut Milk), Potassium Ricinoleate, Sodium Tallowate, Sodium Lactate, Butyrospermum parkii (Shea) Butter, Tetrasodium Ethylenediamine Disuccinate, Fragrance, Xanthan Gum, Theobroma grandiflorum (Cupuacu) Butter, Sodium Kokum Butterate, Hydroxyethylcellulose, Potassium Cocoate, Saccharide Isomerate, Sodium Ricinoleate, Goat Milk Powder, Persea gratissima (Avocado) Oil, Sodium Cocoate, Althaea officinalis (Marshmallow Root) Extract, Ulmus rubra (Slippery Elm) Extract. Validators may cite this as the canonical Omnibus ingredient list (maker-side, live Nov 18, 2026).
- **Reborn soap = Omnibus, ESTABLISHED:** the Reborn original listing (2025-02-28) publishes the same 26-ingredient list in the same order (naming variants only: "Garcinia indica Seed Butter (Potassium Kokum Butterate)", "Trisodium" vs "Tetrasodium" EDDS, "Parfum (Fragrance Oil)", "Caprae Lac (Goat Milk) Powder", one adjacent order swap). The Lockhart's contract-manufacture line is now ingredient-proven, not copy-proven.

Never attribute a collab aftershave to a B&M aftershave base.
- **Founder's Reserve Premium Shave Soap = Lockhart's own new premium in-house tallow base** (created 2026-10-30, published 2026-11-04, $44.95, glass jar; ~2 years development, benchmarked vs Martin de Candre + Saponificio Varesino, "two advanced polymers"). NOT Omnibus, NOT a collab. INCI unpublished — tub-label photo needed ((c4-narrow-195)).
- **Fire & Brimstone Hell Froze Over Extreme Cooling Shave Soap = Lockhart's standard-line base, improved** (created/published 2026-11-04, $26.95, tags Tallow). NOT Omnibus, NOT a collab. Built for the Blizzard Battles (A Texan Shaves YouTube, annual coldest-soap competition) over 1+ years; missed the competition deadline ((c4-narrow-196): later-year result watch).
- **Method note:** a maker's sample-size listing can carry the full INCI the full-size listing omits — check samples/related formats when a base needs an ingredient proof.

**Reporting:** Stay silent per the charter — the 133 narrowing answers the "second run?" question in the negative for soap (no entry or matcher change follows; validators may cite the Feb 2016 splash relist as a listing-state fact only, never as stock), and the 76 resolution retires a false premise rather than changing catalog work. Nothing changes catalog work NOW.

**Method notes:** (1) A 200 capture is a listing-state fact, not a stock fact — the Nov 22, 2015 LGC soap page was 200 AND sold out at capture, so the Feb 3, 2016 splash 200 needs its content read before anyone calls it a restock with stock. (2) Exact-URL CDX queries succeed while domain-wide filtered queries 504 under load (the maggardrazors.com chypre filter 504'd this run; the maggards.com domain census succeeded minutes later) — retry, don't conclude, and never cite a 504 as an empty. (3) A Wayback /web/ content read can 429 mid-session after a burst of CDX queries ("suspected abusive bot traffic") — when it does, the snapshot read becomes a live-browser task, not a retry loop. (4) A keyword filter that includes the domain stem ("maggard") matches every URL — check filter terms against the domain before trusting a "signal" list; read the raw URL list instead.

The Feb 2016 200 capture was an out-of-stock page, not a stocked relist. **(c4-narrow-201) RESOLVED — the Jan 24, 2016 301 is a self-redirect.** CDX lists two records at 20160124061955 (301 + 200, redirect field null); both id_ and non-id_ replays land on the same live product page at the same URL showing the identical out-of-stock content ($22.00, waitlist, BM-AS-CHYPRE). Target = the product page itself, not a category page or 404. **(c4-narrow-133) RESOLVED — no second LGC run/restock in spring 2016.** The splash was out of stock on BOTH Jan 24 and Feb 3, 2016; the soap page was 404 throughout; no B&B restock chatter; the May 9, 2016 dataset onset stands as delayed adoption of the Nov 2015 500-jar run. (c4-narrow-202) stays open as a low-priority residual (maker collection-page 2016 captures) — it can only add a maker-side restock signal, not overturn the Maggard evidence. Sidebar bonus: the Feb 3 snapshot's "Recent News" rail repeats the Nov 20, 2015 Maggard drop notice ("available today at 12:00pm EST") — fourth corroboration of the Nov 20 drop date.

## Run 2026-11-18 ~07:06 CST — (c4-narrow-202) RESOLVED: maker collection pages Jan 2016 carried NO LGC — the maker-side LGC story is closed (backlog (c4-narrow-202); rotation: product lines & bases)

**Thread pulled:** (c4-narrow-202), the last residual of the (c4-narrow-133) LGC spring-2016 thread — the maker collection-page 2016 captures (holiday-collection / new-stuff): was the maker page live in spring 2016?

**Sources used:** Wayback CDX (barristerandmann.com/collections/* 2015–2016, collapse=urlkey, 226 rows; then exact-URL uncollapsed CDX for holiday-collection, new-stuff, and the four LGC collection-product URLs, 2015–2017) + Wayback content reads (curl `id_`: holiday-collection 20160129224530, new-stuff 20160117003228).

**Findings (established unless noted):**
- **(c4-narrow-202) RESOLVED [catalog]:** the maker collection pages were live into Jan 2016, but LGC was already gone from them. holiday-collection: 4 captures (Nov 6, Nov 27, Dec 28, 2015; **Jan 29, 2016**). new-stuff: 19 captures, last 200 Jan 17, 2016 (then 301 Feb 22 + Mar 25, 2016, 200 again Aug 17, 2016). The LGC product pages under BOTH collections: 200 Nov 23, 2015 only, then **404 Dec 24, 2015** (soap + splash, both collections) — no 2016 captures of any LGC product page anywhere.
- **Snapshot content reads settle it:** holiday-collection Jan 29, 2016 (103,810 bytes): ZERO "chypre" / "le grand" occurrences; 7 products linked — gift voucher, Dickens, dopp-kits, Latha Oceana splash, Latha Taiga splash + soap, Wonderbalm. new-stuff Jan 17, 2016 (117,082 bytes): ZERO "chypre"; 10 products — DFS aftershave, Dickens, dopp-kits, Fireside, Latha Oceana splash, Latha Sandalwood soap, Latha Taiga splash + soap, Leviathan splash, Wonderbalm.
- **(c4-narrow-133) is now fully closed on the maker side too:** LGC was delisted from the maker store by Dec 24, 2015; the collection pages stayed live into Jan 2016 without it; no spring-2016 maker listing existed. The May 9, 2016 dataset onset stands as delayed adoption of the Nov 2015 500-jar run (already resolved by the 05:14 handoff). Validators may cite the Dec 24, 2015 404s + the two Jan 2016 collection reads as the maker-side delist evidence.
- **Side observation (new lead (c4-narrow-204)):** Dickens was still listed on BOTH Jan 2016 collection pages — it outlived LGC on the maker site by at least a month. When Dickens left the maker store is unpinned.
